Source: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jNB2L-EJGEU

AI-assisted working draft. AI can make errors. Always check quotations, names, rulings, and other consequential details against the official video.

**[00:10:24.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000021)  
Supervised agencies, colleagues in the Senate, Senator Aimee, magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[00:10:31.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000022)  
Today we will hear the proposed Fiscal Year 2027 budget of the Department of Social Welfare and Development. Sa budget hearing na ito, simple ang prinsipyo natin. Kapag may pamilyang nagugutom, nawawala ng trabaho o kabuhayan, nakakasakit o tinatamaan ng sakuna, dapat may gobyernong sasalo. I’m fully aware that the House-approved GAAB provides P262.757 billion in new appropriations for DSWD, higher than the NEP, although still below the 2026 GAA program level.

**[00:11:07.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000023)  
We take note of the adjustments made by our colleagues in the House of Representatives. AICS increases to about 58.1 billion pesos. The Quick Response Fund is restored to 3 billion pesos. The Sustainable Livelihood Program increases to almost 6 billion pesos. But more money must mean better social protection.

**[00:11:31.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000024)  
Una, we must protect 4Ps as a rights-based investment in poor families and their children. If families graduate from 4Ps, graduation must mean more than losing a grant. They should have a real pathway to education, skills, employment, livelihood, and continued access to basic services. Hindi graduation ang mawalan ng benepisyo.

**[00:11:57.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000025)  
Pangalawa, we must protect our senior citizens.

**[00:12:00.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000026)  
The social pension remains a lifeline for millions of indigent seniors, yet many qualified senior citizens remain on the waiting or remain waiting for assistance.

**[00:12:12.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000027)  
At 1,000 pesos a month, we must also keep asking whether the benefit remains adequate given the rising cost of food, medicine, and transportation.

**[00:12:22.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000028)  
Pangatlo, AICs must remain a genuine social safety net.

**[00:12:27.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000029)  
Ang tulong ay hindi pabor.

**[00:12:31.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000030)  
Dapat nakabatay sa pangangailangan, hindi sa kung sino ang kakilala o kung sino ang nag-refer.

**[00:12:37.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000031)  
We need stronger controls against duplicate payments, but digitization must not exclude people without bank accounts, without a phone, with poor connectivity, or with incomplete records.

**[00:12:53.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000032)  
Pang-apat, we must also prepare for the Super El Niño and other climate shocks.

**[00:12:57.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000033)  
The restoration of the QRF is important, but DSWD must be ready before families reach a crisis point with food assistance, emergency cash, cash for work, livelihood support, and coordination with LGUs.

**[00:13:15.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000034)  
At the same time, we recognize DSWD's investment in disaster response and rehabilitation programs, such as Project Lawa at Binhi,

**[00:13:26.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000035)  
as well as its investment in longer-term climate resilience through the Philippine Community Resilience Project or Panahon ng Pagkilos.

**[00:13:35.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000036)  
Emergency response and long-term resilience must work together,

**[00:13:40.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000037)  
helping communities survive today's shocks while reducing their vulnerability to future disasters.

**[00:13:47.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000038)  
We also welcome the allocation of resources to support vulnerable groups

**[00:13:51.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000039)  
through Enhanced Protect Teen, Project Aruga, Project SAFE,

**[00:13:57.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000040)  
and the Special Committee on LGBTQIA Plus Affairs,

**[00:14:01.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000041)

**[00:14:07.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000042)

**[00:14:10.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000043)

**[00:14:21.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000046)

And mas malaki pa yung nawawalang pera

**[00:20:40.160] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000221)

**[00:20:41.920] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000222)

**[00:20:44.360] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000223)

**[00:20:46.080] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000224)

**[00:20:47.380] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000225)

**[00:20:49.920] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000226)

**[00:20:51.620] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000227)

**[00:20:53.100] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000228)

**[00:20:55.940] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000229)

**[00:21:00.060] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000231)

**[00:21:05.380] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000233)  
Dapat mayroon tayong

**[00:21:06.300] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000234)

**[00:21:08.320] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000235)

**[00:21:10.060] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000236)

**[00:21:12.640] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000237)  
Yung tunay na PWD.

**[00:21:14.760] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000238)  
Araw-araw na nag-ooperate

**[00:21:16.720] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000239)

**[00:21:18.220] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000240)  
Araw-araw na

**[00:21:19.920] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000241)

**[00:21:22.200] RestoPH President David Sison** (asr-000242)  
yung gumagamit.

**[00:21:27.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000243)  
Selling of fake PWDs

**[00:21:29.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000244)

**[00:21:31.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000245)

**[00:21:33.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000246)  
Mayroon din mga accounts sa Carousel

**[00:21:35.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000247)  
at Facebook na nag-offer ng

**[00:21:37.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000248)

**[00:21:39.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000249)  
Meron pang package.

**[00:21:42.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000250)  
Yung iba pa nga

**[00:21:43.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000251)  
ay may kasama pang booklets.

**[00:21:47.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000252)  
Pwede natin pakita yung

**[00:21:47.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000253)  
slides. Ayan po.

**[00:21:49.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000254)  
Talamak ang bendahan ng mga

**[00:21:51.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000255)  
PWD IDs

**[00:21:52.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000256)  
sa Facebook.

**[00:21:56.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000257)  
Anong ginagawa ng

**[00:21:57.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000258)  
NCDA?

**[00:22:00.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000259)  
Ito pong masaklap, Madam Chair.

**[00:22:03.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000260)  
Maraming kakilala

**[00:22:03.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000261)  
na pagkalaki-laki

**[00:22:06.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000262)  
ng katawan,

**[00:22:07.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000263)  
tambay sa basketball

**[00:22:08.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000264)  
at meron siyang PWD ID.

**[00:22:12.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000265)  
Lalaaki ito. Tinanong ko siya, pare,

**[00:22:13.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000266)  
ba't meron kang PWD ID?

**[00:22:16.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000267)  
Ano yung kapansana mo?

**[00:22:17.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000268)  
Hindi, psychosocial ako.

**[00:22:19.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000269)  
Totoo yan. Napakarami

**[00:22:21.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000270)  
mga tao ang lalaki ng katawan

**[00:22:22.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000271)  
may mga magagandang trabaho

**[00:22:25.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000272)  
and yet may PWD ID.

**[00:22:26.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000273)  
Paano?

**[00:22:27.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000274)  
Mga kalusot mga ito.

**[00:22:28.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000275)  
Of course, walang problema sa akin

**[00:22:30.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000276)  
kung talagang tunay na may kapansanan.

**[00:22:31.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000277)  
Siguro it's time na we have to define

**[00:22:33.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000278)  
ano ba talaga

**[00:22:34.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000279)  
ang mga requirements bago makatanggap

**[00:22:38.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000280)  
ng PWD ID ang isang tao.

**[00:22:40.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000281)  
Anong klaseng makapansanan?

**[00:22:41.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000282)  
Kasi halos lahat mga kilala ko

**[00:22:44.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000283)  
na may PWD ID na ang lalaki ng katawan

**[00:22:47.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000284)  
nakalagay doon psychosocial.

**[00:22:50.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000285)  
Meron pa nga sabi ko,

**[00:22:51.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000286)  
ba't ka merong PWD ID?

**[00:22:53.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000287)  
Kasi may sinusitis ako eh.

**[00:22:54.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000288)  
At binigyan ng doktor.

**[00:22:57.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000289)  
Meron pa ako alam, basketballista.

**[00:22:59.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000290)  
Mga basketball player, Madam Chair,

**[00:23:02.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000291)  
medyo nabalian lang.

**[00:23:04.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000292)  
Pupunta ng hospital.

**[00:23:05.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000293)  
O, pera ng doktor.

**[00:23:06.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000294)  
Gusto mo, bigyan kita ang PWD ID.

**[00:23:07.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000295)  
Sige, bigyan.

**[00:23:08.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000296)  
And then permanently,

**[00:23:10.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000297)  
permanent na yung PWD ID.

**[00:23:12.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000298)  
Naabuso po yung PWD ID

**[00:23:16.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000299)  
at ang kawawa po dito

**[00:23:17.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000300)  
yung mga malilit na tindahan,

**[00:23:19.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000301)  
restaurant.

**[00:23:20.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000302)  
Saan ka nakikita yung isang tao

**[00:23:23.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000303)  
na magandang trabaho,

**[00:23:26.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000304)  
malaking katawan,

**[00:23:27.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000305)  
o punta sa restaurant

**[00:23:28.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000306)  
o order ng lobster, crabs,

**[00:23:30.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000307)  
para makuha ng 20%.

**[00:23:31.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000308)  
Tapos niya naakit sa hotel,

**[00:23:33.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000309)  
mag-check-in,

**[00:23:34.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000310)  
20% na naman.

**[00:23:35.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000311)  
Naabuso po talaga itong PWD ID

**[00:23:37.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000312)  
at ang nakikita kong problema rito,

**[00:23:39.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000313)  
siguro from now on,

**[00:23:40.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000314)  
meron kasi mga PWD ID na legit.

**[00:23:45.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000315)  
Pero fake naman yung cardholder.

**[00:23:49.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000316)  
Wala naman talagang sakit.

**[00:23:50.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000317)  
Pero mas marami yung fake na PWD ID.

**[00:23:53.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000318)  
It's about time that we have to address this issue

**[00:23:56.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000319)  
na para naman mabawasan yung pamimaking ng PWD ID

**[00:23:59.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000320)  
at mabigyan yung karapat-dapat talaga

**[00:24:02.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000321)  
na mga taong may kapansanan.

**[00:24:05.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000322)  
Kasi ang premise naman talaga ng batas

**[00:24:08.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000323)  
kaya nagkaroon tayo ng discount sa mga PWDs

**[00:24:12.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000324)  
ay sila ay matulungan,

**[00:24:15.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000325)  
mapagaan yung kanilang kalagayan

**[00:24:16.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000326)  
and most of them walang trabaho,

**[00:24:19.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000327)  
hindi makapagtrabaho.

**[00:24:20.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000328)  
Makapagtrabaho man, part-time lang

**[00:24:22.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000329)  
and then malaking gastusin sa gamot.

**[00:24:26.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000330)  
Pero paano itong mga taong alam ko

**[00:24:30.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000331)  
may mga trabaho above minimum

**[00:24:33.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000332)  
ang lalaki ng katawan

**[00:24:34.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000333)  
and yet nagkakaroon ng fake PWD IDs?

**[00:24:38.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000334)  
Ang tanong nasa National Center for Disability Affairs

**[00:24:41.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000335)  
kayong hensyang natasang siguraduhin

**[00:24:44.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000336)  
maayos ang implementasyon ng lahat ng batas

**[00:24:46.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000337)  
patungkol sa PWDs

**[00:24:47.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000338)  
pero mga inutil kayo.

**[00:24:50.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000339)  
Sobrang inutil as a matter of fact.

**[00:24:53.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000340)  
Bakit umabot sa katakot-takot

**[00:24:54.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000341)  
na 6 million ng mga faking IDs?

**[00:24:56.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000342)  
Paano nakalusot ito?

**[00:24:58.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000343)  
Wala ba kayong koordinasyon

**[00:24:59.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000344)  
with other agencies at sa LGUs?

**[00:25:02.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000345)  
Wala ba kayong ngipin

**[00:25:03.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000346)  
upang masigurang implementasyon ng batas?

**[00:25:06.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000347)  
Bakit wala kayong kakayahan

**[00:25:07.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000348)  
na imonitor ang problemang ito

**[00:25:10.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000349)  
na nagsimula pa noong pandemic?

**[00:25:13.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000350)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:25:14.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000351)  
my purpose is to uphold the rights

**[00:25:16.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000352)  
and protect persons with disabilities

**[00:25:18.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000353)  
not to discredit them.

**[00:25:21.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000354)  
Nais kumatsyak na matatanggap

**[00:25:23.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000355)  
ng mga liitimong PWD

**[00:25:24.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000356)  
ang mga binipisyong

**[00:25:25.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000357)  
itinakda ng batas.

**[00:25:28.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000358)  
Hindi makatarungan maging dahilan

**[00:25:29.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000359)  
ang pamemeke ng mga scammer

**[00:25:31.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000360)  
upang pagdudahan ang pagbibigay ng diskwento

**[00:25:34.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000361)  
sa mga tunay na nangangailangan nito.

**[00:25:37.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000362)  
We may also need to review

**[00:25:38.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000363)  
existing laws to clarify

**[00:25:40.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000364)  
which disabilities

**[00:25:42.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000365)  
qualify for government benefits.

**[00:25:46.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000366)  
These benefits are grounded

**[00:25:47.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000367)  
in social justice

**[00:25:48.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000368)  
and the Philippine Constitution

**[00:25:49.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000369)  
which affirms the dignity

**[00:25:51.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000370)  
of every person and guarantees

**[00:25:54.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000371)  
full respect for human rights.

**[00:25:55.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000372)  
Huwag nating gamitin sa kalokohan

**[00:25:58.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000373)  
ang talagayan ng ating mga kababayang

**[00:25:59.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000374)  
may kapansanan.

**[00:26:02.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000375)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[00:26:04.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000376)  
Maraming salamat din po,

**[00:26:05.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000377)  
Senator Rafi.

**[00:26:06.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000378)  
Secretary Rex,

**[00:26:07.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000379)  
I'm sure you will touch on this

**[00:26:10.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000380)  
sa presentation nyo

**[00:26:11.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000381)  
including in relation to DOJ,

**[00:26:14.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000382)  
Department of Health,

**[00:26:15.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000383)  
and the LGUs na binanggit din po

**[00:26:17.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000384)  
sa pag-raise nitong importanteng isyong ito.

**[00:26:20.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000385)  
Salamat, Secretary Rex.

**[00:26:21.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000386)  
Senator Erwin,

**[00:26:22.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000387)  
gusto nyo po bang mag-opening remarks?

**[00:26:23.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000388)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:26:25.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000389)  
Una po sa lahat,

**[00:26:28.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000390)  
magandang hapon po sa lahat,

**[00:26:30.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000391)  
lalo na po sa ating

**[00:26:32.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000392)  
Chairperson, Senadora Risa Hontiveros.

**[00:26:35.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000393)  
Of course, sa aking mga kasamahan po,

**[00:26:37.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000394)  
Senador Rafi at Senador Aimee,

**[00:26:40.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000395)  
kay Secretary Rex Gatchalian,

**[00:26:42.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000396)  
at mga kasamahan ko po,

**[00:26:44.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000397)  
tayo po na nasa pamahalaan

**[00:26:46.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000398)  
at sa ating mga kababayan

**[00:26:49.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000399)  
na anunod sa atin ngayon.

**[00:26:51.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000400)  
Today, as we deliberate

**[00:26:52.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000401)  
the proposed 2027 budget

**[00:26:54.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000402)  
of the Department of Social,

**[00:26:55.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000403)  
Welfare, and Development,

**[00:26:57.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000404)  
let us be reminded

**[00:26:58.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000405)  
that it holds a vital place

**[00:26:59.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000406)  
in our government.

**[00:27:01.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000407)  
As a lead agency

**[00:27:02.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000408)  
in social protection,

**[00:27:03.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000409)  
DSWD serves as the ultimate safety net

**[00:27:07.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000410)  
for our poor families,

**[00:27:08.820] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000411)  
children,

**[00:27:09.920] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000412)  
senior citizens,

**[00:27:10.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000413)  
persons with disabilities,

**[00:27:12.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000414)  
and individuals facing crises.

**[00:27:15.860] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000415)  
Sa DSWD po,

**[00:27:17.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000416)  
nakasalalay ang pag-asa

**[00:27:18.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000417)  
ng ating mga kababayan

**[00:27:19.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000418)  
kapag sila ay nadadapa

**[00:27:20.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000419)  
sa gitna man ng kahirapan,

**[00:27:23.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000420)  
sakuna,

**[00:27:23.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000421)  
o bigla ang krisis sa buhay.

**[00:27:25.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000422)  
At sa mga kababayan,

**[00:27:25.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000423)  
For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:27:28.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000424)  
the DSWD's proposed budget

**[00:27:30.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000425)  
under GAB stands at

**[00:27:31.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000426)  
P262.8 billion,

**[00:27:34.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000427)  
reflecting significant adjustments.

**[00:27:37.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000428)  
Pero,

**[00:27:38.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000429)  
tila kulang pa,

**[00:27:39.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000430)  
may paparating po tayo na El Niño,

**[00:27:42.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000431)  
hindi pa tapos ang

**[00:27:43.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000432)  
gera sa Middle East,

**[00:27:46.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000433)  
at ang DSWD kasi po

**[00:27:47.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000434)  
ang nasa front line sa pagbibigay

**[00:27:49.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000435)  
po ng ayuda pag may sakuna,

**[00:27:51.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000436)  
dilubyo, at gera.

**[00:27:54.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000437)  
Sasapat

**[00:27:55.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000438)  
ba ang pondo nila

**[00:27:56.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000439)  
para sa ika nga

**[00:27:57.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000440)  
mga paparating na emergency.

**[00:28:00.800] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000441)  
While we recognize

**[00:28:01.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000442)  
the need for fiscal prudence,

**[00:28:03.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000443)  
this committee must meticulously review

**[00:28:05.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000444)  
where these cuts are being made.

**[00:28:07.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000445)  
Tungkulin po natin

**[00:28:08.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000446)  
na siguraduhin

**[00:28:09.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000447)  
hindi mapapawasan

**[00:28:10.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000448)  
ng direktang tulong

**[00:28:11.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000449)  
o serbisyo

**[00:28:12.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000450)  
para sa mahihirap.

**[00:28:14.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000451)  
Dapat din po natin

**[00:28:14.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000452)  
siguraduhin

**[00:28:15.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000453)  
na ang pondo ng bayan

**[00:28:16.800] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000454)  
ay may kaaktibat

**[00:28:17.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000455)  
na pananagutan at resulta.

**[00:28:20.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000456)  
Are our resources

**[00:28:21.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000457)  
reaching the right beneficiaries

**[00:28:22.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000458)  
at the right time

**[00:28:23.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000459)  
and in the right amount?

**[00:28:25.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000460)  
How do we ensure

**[00:28:26.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000461)  
the programs

**[00:28:26.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000462)  
such as AICS complement

**[00:28:28.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000463)  
rather than undermine

**[00:28:29.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000464)  
long-term poverty reduction?

**[00:28:32.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000465)  
Are our investments

**[00:28:32.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000466)  
in the Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program,

**[00:28:35.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000467)  
social pension,

**[00:28:36.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000468)  
and sustainable livelihood programs

**[00:28:38.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000469)  
sufficient to help beneficiaries

**[00:28:40.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000470)  
achieve lasting improvements

**[00:28:42.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000471)  
in their lives?

**[00:28:43.860] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000472)  
Ang bawat piso nilaan po

**[00:28:45.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000473)  
sa DSWD

**[00:28:46.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000474)  
ay dapat may katumbas

**[00:28:47.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000475)  
na tunay na tulong,

**[00:28:48.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000476)  
malinaw na pananagutan

**[00:28:49.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000477)  
at kongkretong pagbabago

**[00:28:51.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000478)  
sa buhay ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:28:53.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000479)  
As we scrutinize this budget,

**[00:28:55.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000480)  
our focus will be

**[00:28:56.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000481)  
on resolving critical operational

**[00:28:57.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000482)  
and implementation issues.

**[00:29:00.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000483)  
We need clear justification

**[00:29:02.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000484)  
for significant budget cuts

**[00:29:04.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000485)  
in key social safety net programs,

**[00:29:07.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000486)  
particularly the AICS,

**[00:29:10.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000487)  
whose funding and targeted beneficiaries

**[00:29:12.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000488)  
have been substantially reduced.

**[00:29:15.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000489)  
We will look into the status

**[00:29:16.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000490)  
of four P's

**[00:29:17.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000491)  
under recent policy adjustment,

**[00:29:19.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000492)  
as well as the social pension program,

**[00:29:22.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000493)  
where over half a million

**[00:29:23.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000494)  
eligible indigent seniors

**[00:29:25.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000495)  
and citizens remain wait-listed

**[00:29:27.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000496)  
due to budget constraints.

**[00:29:29.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000497)  
We must address recurring

**[00:29:31.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000498)  
COA observations regarding

**[00:29:32.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000499)  
database inaccuracies,

**[00:29:34.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000500)  
delayed disbursement,

**[00:29:36.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000501)  
unliquidated cash advances,

**[00:29:38.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000502)  
and inclusion errors.

**[00:29:39.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000503)  
Bawat piso po ng pondo

**[00:29:42.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000504)  
ay dapat makarating

**[00:29:43.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000505)  
sa totoong nangangailangan

**[00:29:44.840] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000506)  
ng walang delay

**[00:29:45.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000507)  
at walang pong anomalya.

**[00:29:48.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000508)  
We are here today

**[00:29:49.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000509)  
not to fault-find,

**[00:29:50.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000510)  
but to ensure that DSWD

**[00:29:52.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000511)  
is given a balanced,

**[00:29:53.820] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000512)  
efficient,

**[00:29:54.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000513)  
and responsive budget.

**[00:29:56.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000514)  
Gusto pong masiguro

**[00:29:58.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000515)  
ng representasyong ito

**[00:30:00.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000516)  
na ang bawat piso sa pondo ng DSWD ay magagamit nating tama, mabilis at may tunay na malasakit sa taong bayan.

**[00:30:09.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000517)  
Let us ensure that the DSWD budget is not only compassionate in purpose but also efficient, accountable and impactful in practice.

**[00:30:18.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000518)  
Madam Chair, the issues raised by Senator Raffi earlier was discussed already by our Committee on Social Justice and Welfare last Monday

**[00:30:28.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000519)  
where we discussed lifetime validity of PWD IDs.

**[00:30:33.840] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000520)  
Maybe the NCDA can reiterate their position regarding these fake IDs.

**[00:30:39.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000521)  
We look forward to a constructive and transparent budget presentation from DSWD.

**[00:30:44.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000522)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[00:30:46.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000523)  
Maraming salamat din po, Senator and former Secretary Erwin.

**[00:30:49.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000524)  
Thank you.

**[00:30:50.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000525)  
So, Secretary Rex, you have the floor now to lead the presentation.

**[00:30:54.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000526)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Madam Chair, I will go through the top line of the budget.

**[00:30:58.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000527)  
And then, if you will allow me, I will go straight into the pressing matter on PWD IDs.

**[00:31:05.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000528)  
Yes, please do.

**[00:31:05.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000529)  
Madam Chairperson and our budget sponsor, Senator Riza Antiveros, honorable members of the Committee,

**[00:31:10.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000530)  
co-workers in DSWD, ladies and gentlemen, good afternoon.

**[00:31:13.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000531)  
The DSWD, including its attached and supervised agencies, is honored to appear before the Senate

**[00:31:18.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000532)  
as part of our accountability for the public resources entrusted to us.

**[00:31:23.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000533)  
In the next slides, we will present our annual budget utilization performance,

**[00:31:27.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000534)  
the proposed budget for 2027, and the status of major programs implemented by the DSWD.

**[00:31:34.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000535)  
For 2027, the DSWD has a total budget of P240.7 billion under the National Expenditure Program.

**[00:31:41.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000536)  
Of this amount, P235.7 billion is allocated to the DSWD Office of the Secretary.

**[00:31:47.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000537)  
For our attached and supervised agencies, the proposed budgets are shown on the screen.

**[00:31:51.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000538)  
Compared to the 2026 General Appropriations Act, the 2027 NEP for the DSWD,

**[00:31:56.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000539)  
including its attached and supervised,

**[00:31:57.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000540)  
agencies reflect lower levels, with the exception of NACC and NCSC,

**[00:32:03.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000541)  
whose budget increases are under personal services because of new positions created on or filled up.

**[00:32:10.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000542)  
For 2025, the DBM released approximately P254 billion to the DSWD,

**[00:32:15.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000543)  
both for our agency budget and releases from other fund sources.

**[00:32:19.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000544)  
We are pleased to report that the DSWD achieved a near 100% utilization rate as of September 2026.

**[00:32:25.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000545)  
For 2026, the DSWD has so far utilized

**[00:32:27.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000546)  
81% of the agency's specific budget of P264.3 billion.

**[00:32:34.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000547)  
For the Calamity Fund and the Quick Response Fund replenishments,

**[00:32:37.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000548)  
83.71% of the P13.7 billion released so far has already been utilized.

**[00:32:43.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000549)  
Beyond the budget figures, these investments reflect the very essence of DSWD's work,

**[00:32:48.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000550)  
investing in human capital development, strengthening community-driven development,

**[00:32:52.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000551)  
and ensuring a rapid disaster response so that the gains of the development

**[00:32:57.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000552)  
are not undone by disasters and other shocks.

**[00:33:00.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000556)  
As social risk and vulnerabilities evolve, the DSWD implements innovative flagship programs

**[00:33:04.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000557)  
and new approaches to address emerging social concerns and response to changing needs of Filipino families.

**[00:33:11.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000558)  
Walang Gutom addresses food insecurity, Tarabasa supports children's learning,

**[00:33:15.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000559)  
while providing cash-for-work opportunities for the youth,

**[00:33:18.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000560)  
and Pag-Abot reaches people in street situations,

**[00:33:21.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000561)  
providing immediate food assistance through the Walang Gutom Kitchen,

**[00:33:23.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000562)  
and connecting them to appropriate social protection services.

**[00:33:27.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000563)  
Together, these programs invest in people, respond to their specific needs, and help them build more dignified and sustainable lives.

**[00:33:35.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000564)  
We are also?

**[00:33:36.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000564-split-2)  
Sec. Rex, if you could pause for a bit. Tech, pakiayusin natin yung slides ni Secretary. Parang nag-sign out daw. Okay. Salamat. Please proceed, Sec. Rex.

**[00:33:47.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000564-split-3)  
We are also developing targeted responses to prevalent and social welfare concerns.

**[00:33:52.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000565)  
These include initiatives such as Project Aruga, Protect Teen, and Project Safe for Osaic Victims.

**[00:33:58.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000566)  
These initiatives address the specific needs of children, adolescents, and families, strengthening our capacity to protect those at risk and deliver appropriate timely support.

**[00:34:08.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000567)  
While we continue to innovate and respond to new challenges, we remain fully grounded in our core mandate, delivering regular programs and services that millions of Filipinos depend on.

**[00:34:17.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000568)  
At the heart of these efforts is our commitment to invest in people.

**[00:34:21.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000569)  
Helping children.

**[00:34:22.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000570)  
Helping children grow, families become more self-reliant, and communities move forward.

**[00:34:27.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000571)  
This is what social protection must deliver.

**[00:34:29.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000572)  
Immediate support when people need it the most, and sustained opportunities to build a better future.

**[00:34:34.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000573)  
Let me now turn to our major programs, beginning with Pantawid Pamilang Pilipino Program, or 4Ps, our country's flagship investment in human capital development.

**[00:34:44.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000574)  
In 2025, we utilized 63.6 billion or 63.7 billion allotment, or 99.83%.

**[00:34:52.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000576)  
To pay the conditional cash grants due to the 3.9 million beneficiaries.

**[00:34:57.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000577)  
For 2026, our 112.9 billion allotment includes 98.3 billion for grants, with obligation and disbursement progressing according to scheduled payments.

**[00:35:07.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000578)  
For 2027, 99.1 billion budget, including 81.3 billion for grants, will continue to support 3.5 million households, sustaining investments in health, education, and family development,

**[00:35:21.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000579)  
with focus on poor and near-poor families.

**[00:35:25.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000580)  
Beyond budget utilization, we measure 4Ps by its results and ensure that our programs are guided by evidence.

**[00:35:30.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000581)  
From 2022 to July 2026, 1.77 million households graduated to self-sufficiency, 7.24 million children completed primary and secondary education, and 78,886 beneficiaries passed licensure examinations,

**[00:35:49.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000582)  
including 259 top-notchers.

**[00:35:53.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000583)  
Under the F1KD First 1,000 Days grant, 83.9% of pregnant beneficiaries nationwide completed the required prenatal visits.

**[00:36:03.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000584)  
These results are reinforced by independent studies.

**[00:36:06.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000585)  
The World Bank's 2026 study attributed a 2.2% point reduction in national poverty to 4Ps and estimated a 4.4 cost-benefit ratio.

**[00:36:17.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000586)  
The Brain Trust Study also recognizes the program's educational impact.

**[00:36:22.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000587)  
in its reach among poor households.

**[00:36:24.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000588)  
These findings strengthen our commitment to evidence-informed policymaking, continuous program improvement, and ensuring that the public resources deliver measurable results for Filipino families.

**[00:36:36.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000589)  
Under protective services, AICS remains our primary program for individuals and families in crisis, complemented by community-based services for vulnerable sectors.

**[00:36:44.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000590)  
In 2025, we utilize 100% of the budget, assisting over 9.4 million beneficiaries.

**[00:36:51.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000591)  
For 2026,

**[00:36:52.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000592)  
utilization has reached almost 92%,

**[00:36:54.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000593)  
serving 6.7 million beneficiaries to date.

**[00:36:58.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000594)  
Under the President's Uplift Directive,

**[00:37:00.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000595)  
we have also assisted 5.8 million beneficiaries

**[00:37:03.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000596)  
as of October 14.

**[00:37:05.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000597)  
For 2027, the 33.3 billion,

**[00:37:08.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000598)  
including 29.4 billion for subsidies,

**[00:37:11.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000599)  
will help individuals and families in crisis

**[00:37:13.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000600)  
meet urgent needs,

**[00:37:15.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000601)  
access essential services,

**[00:37:16.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000602)  
and recover from financial and personal hardships.

**[00:37:19.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000603)  
However, additional resources

**[00:37:21.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000604)  
may be needed to keep pace

**[00:37:22.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000605)  
with the growing demand

**[00:37:23.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000606)  
and sustain these outcomes.

**[00:37:25.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000607)  
Your Honors,

**[00:37:25.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000608)  
AICS is evolving into an instrument

**[00:37:27.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000609)  
for adaptive and shock-responsive social protection.

**[00:37:31.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000610)  
Under the President's Uplift Initiative,

**[00:37:33.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000611)  
we provided 5,000 cash assistance

**[00:37:35.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000612)  
to 1.839 million transport workers.

**[00:37:40.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000613)  
A rapid SWS assessment

**[00:37:43.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000614)  
found that 96% considered the grant crucial

**[00:37:47.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000615)  
to keeping them on the road,

**[00:37:48.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000616)  
while 80% use it for food,

**[00:37:51.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000617)  
while we also provided top-up support

**[00:37:53.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000618)  
for existing beneficiaries

**[00:37:55.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000619)  
and extended aid to vulnerable households

**[00:37:57.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000620)  
identified through CBMS data.

**[00:37:59.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000621)  
This demonstrates how AICS can scale up support

**[00:38:02.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000622)  
and reach families vulnerable to economic shocks

**[00:38:05.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000623)  
when they need it the most.

**[00:38:06.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000624)  
Another key investment is the social protection pension

**[00:38:09.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000625)  
for indigent senior citizens,

**[00:38:12.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000626)  
providing vital income support to our older persons.

**[00:38:16.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000627)  
In 2025, we serve 4.11 million beneficiaries.

**[00:38:19.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000628)  
For 2026,

**[00:38:21.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000629)  
utilization is at 88%

**[00:38:23.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000630)  
as we enroll new beneficiaries

**[00:38:24.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000631)  
with 51 billion available to expand coverage.

**[00:38:27.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000632)  
For 2027,

**[00:38:29.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000633)  
the 51.6 billion budget allots 50 billion for subsidies.

**[00:38:34.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000634)  
We would like to flag that based on CBMS data,

**[00:38:37.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000635)  
at least 550,000 older seniors,

**[00:38:41.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000636)  
older persons remain unserved

**[00:38:43.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000637)  
and we hope to extend social pension

**[00:38:45.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000638)  
to these senior citizens.

**[00:38:46.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000639)  
For social pension,

**[00:38:47.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000640)  
we are making implementation simpler,

**[00:38:49.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000641)  
fairer, and more responsive,

**[00:38:50.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000642)  
in support of the progressive realization

**[00:38:53.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000643)  
of universal social pension.

**[00:38:55.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000644)  
First, we removed the requirement

**[00:38:57.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000645)  
to provide frailty or sickliness eligibility.

**[00:39:02.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000646)  
Eligibility is now based on age,

**[00:39:04.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000647)  
with enrollment prioritized by age.

**[00:39:07.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000648)  
Second, we shifted to data-driven targeting

**[00:39:10.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000649)  
using CBMS data,

**[00:39:11.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000650)  
cross-checked with SSS, GSIS, and PVOW,

**[00:39:15.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000651)  
to ensure applicants are not active pensioners.

**[00:39:18.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000652)  
To reach those who may be missed

**[00:39:20.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000653)  
by the field

**[00:39:20.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000654)  
or those who may not be included in the program,

**[00:39:23.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000655)  
we also allowed applications

**[00:39:25.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000656)  
and LGU referrals for persons aged 70 and above,

**[00:39:29.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000657)  
subject to validation with DSA registry data.

**[00:39:33.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000658)  
Finally, we are advancing the silver economy across ASEAN,

**[00:39:37.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000659)  
recognizing older persons

**[00:39:38.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000660)  
not only as beneficiaries to social protection,

**[00:39:41.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000661)  
but also as contributors to communities and the economy.

**[00:39:44.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000662)  
The supplementary feeding program provides support,

**[00:39:47.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000663)  
supports the nutrition needs of children

**[00:39:48.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000664)  
in child development centers,

**[00:39:50.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000665)  
and supervised neighborhood place.

**[00:39:52.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000666)  
For 2027, the 6.4 billion budget

**[00:39:55.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000667)  
will provide supplementary meals to 1.899 million children,

**[00:39:59.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000668)  
sustaining coverage at the first fiscal year 2026 target.

**[00:40:03.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000669)  
The lower allocation reflects a shorter feeding period

**[00:40:07.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000670)  
of 120 days compared with 180 days in 2026,

**[00:40:11.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000671)  
not a reduction of children served.

**[00:40:14.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000672)  
Social protection must create pathways to self-reliance.

**[00:40:18.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000673)  
Through the Sustainable Livelihood program,

**[00:40:19.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000674)  
we help families in rural areas,

**[00:40:20.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000675)  
families generate income and achieve economic independence.

**[00:40:23.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000676)  
In 2025, SLP assisted 275,478 households exceeding its target

**[00:40:29.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000677)  
with 100% of its 6.2 allocation obligated as of September 2026.

**[00:40:37.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000678)  
238,233 households have been assisted.

**[00:40:40.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000679)  
For 2027, the 5.1 billion budget will support 251,449 households

**[00:40:47.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000680)  
in developing sustainable livelihoods, generating income,

**[00:40:50.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000681)  
and achieving greater economic self-reliance.

**[00:40:53.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000682)  
Given the program's performance and reach,

**[00:40:55.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000683)  
additional funding may be needed to extend these opportunities to more families.

**[00:40:59.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000684)  
We have revamped SLP to move beyond one-time assistance

**[00:41:03.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000685)  
towards sustainable enterprise development.

**[00:41:07.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000686)  
Through Pag-CBOL, we provide long-term support and sustainability plans

**[00:41:11.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000687)  
to help livelihood grow into viable sources of income.

**[00:41:14.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000688)  
We also strengthen market linkages

**[00:41:16.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000689)  
and open new sales channels including digital platforms like TikTok.

**[00:41:20.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000690)  
Our goal is clear, invest in livelihoods that can grow, compete,

**[00:41:24.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000691)  
and provide sustainable income for families.

**[00:41:26.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000692)  
A consistent part of our mandate is to provide residential and center-based services

**[00:41:30.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000693)  
for individuals who require more intensive and sustained care.

**[00:41:34.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000694)  
These include children, older persons, persons with disabilities,

**[00:41:37.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000695)  
and other individuals whose circumstances require specific intervention.

**[00:41:41.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000696)  
For 2025, we serve 9,620 clients,

**[00:41:46.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000697)  
exceeding the target by 11.34%,

**[00:41:49.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000698)  
while in 2026, 8,674 clients have already been served as of this reporting period.

**[00:41:57.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000699)  
For 2027, the 3.3 billion budget will provide specialized care and support

**[00:42:03.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000700)  
to 8,640 clients, helping meet their needs and improve their well-being.

**[00:42:08.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000701)  
Our challenge is to ensure that our 77 residential and center-based facilities

**[00:42:13.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000702)  
remain safe, appropriate, accessible, and sustainable.

**[00:42:16.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000703)  
We are also capable of delivering quality social welfare services.

**[00:42:19.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000704)  
It is for this reason that we are proposing P286.1 million

**[00:42:23.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000705)  
for power system rehabilitation of our care facilities.

**[00:42:27.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000706)  
To address overcapacity, we are also seeking an additional P204 million

**[00:42:31.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000707)  
for cost-of-care subsidies to qualified private social welfare agencies,

**[00:42:36.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000708)  
50 of which have already been operating beyond their bed capacity.

**[00:42:41.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000709)  
These investments will improve care facilities

**[00:42:43.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000710)  
and expand our capacity to serve vulnerable clients.

**[00:42:46.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000711)  
On disaster response, the QRS allows DSWD to provide timely assistance

**[00:42:51.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000712)  
before, during, and after emergencies,

**[00:42:53.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000713)  
ensuring that affected families feel the government's presence

**[00:42:56.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000714)  
when they need it the most.

**[00:42:58.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000715)  
We fully utilize the 2025 allotment and replenishment.

**[00:43:02.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000716)  
For 2026, we have utilized 14.3 billion of the 16.7 billion release.

**[00:43:08.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000717)  
Our strategy is always present where disasters strike

**[00:43:12.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000718)  
with relief goods ready for immediate deployment.

**[00:43:14.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000719)  
We have 1,048 warehouses nationwide

**[00:43:18.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000720)  
with 4.4 million family food packs in stock as of September 30, 2026.

**[00:43:24.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000721)  
Our modernized facilities can produce up to 63,000 food packs per shift

**[00:43:28.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000722)  
with 7.2 million food packs produced in 2025.

**[00:43:33.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000723)  
We also provide emergency cash transfers and cash for work in 2025.

**[00:43:37.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000724)  
1.3 million families benefited from or receive help

**[00:43:41.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000725)  
to meet urgent needs and begin recovery.

**[00:43:43.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000726)  
For 2027,

**[00:43:44.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000727)  
the 1.9 billion budget for disaster response and rehabilitation

**[00:43:48.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000728)  
will continue the implementation of LAWA and BINHI,

**[00:43:52.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000729)  
which provides cash support for temporary employment

**[00:43:56.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000730)  
and skills training to poor and disadvantaged families

**[00:43:59.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000731)  
vulnerable to climate hazards, including El Ni?o-induced drought.

**[00:44:04.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000732)  
Drought requires a whole-of-government approach.

**[00:44:06.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000733)  
DSWD provides social protection and humanitarian assistance

**[00:44:09.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000734)  
to meet urgent needs, protect food security and help affected families

**[00:44:13.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000735)  
recover and restore their livelihoods.

**[00:44:14.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000736)  
This includes complementing the efforts of partner agencies and LGUs.

**[00:44:19.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000737)  
This slide will show practical components of LAWA and BINHI,

**[00:44:22.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000738)  
protecting water access, sustaining food production,

**[00:44:25.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000739)  
and strengthening livelihoods.

**[00:44:27.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000740)  
The approach is intended to help communities address immediate effects of El Nino

**[00:44:31.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000741)  
while building capacity to withstand future shocks.

**[00:44:34.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000742)  
The department's role is complemented by coordination with relevant partners,

**[00:44:38.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000743)  
particularly for technical support and provision of agriculture inputs

**[00:44:41.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000744)  
and other sector-driven assistance.

**[00:44:43.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000745)  
In addition,

**[00:44:44.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000746)  
the department also uses its quick response fund for emergency cash transfer.

**[00:44:49.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000747)  
It provides temporary unconditional cash support to individuals and families

**[00:44:53.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000748)  
affected by crises, disaster, and economic shocks.

**[00:44:56.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000749)  
This slide presents assistance for affected

**[00:45:00.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000750)  
farmers in Regions 3 and 5

**[00:45:02.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000751)  
including 77,260

**[00:45:04.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000752)  
farmers in Region 3

**[00:45:06.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000753)  
and 26,041

**[00:45:09.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000754)  
in Region 5.

**[00:45:10.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000755)  
The grant is calculated

**[00:45:12.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000756)  
for 30 days up to a maximum

**[00:45:13.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000757)  
of 90 days based on 75

**[00:45:15.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000758)  
of regional minimum wage.

**[00:45:18.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000759)  
Madam Chair, that ends the presentation and if you

**[00:45:20.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000760)  
will allow me to immediately answer

**[00:45:22.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000761)  
or put on record the response

**[00:45:23.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000762)  
to the PWD concerns.

**[00:45:26.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000763)  
Salamat, Sec. Rex, but before you do

**[00:45:28.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000764)  
that, the Chair would like to recognize

**[00:45:29.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000765)  
the presence of the Senate President, Senator

**[00:45:31.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000766)  
Sherwin. Salamat sa inyong presensya.

**[00:45:34.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000767)  
Well, after Sec. Rex

**[00:45:36.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000768)  
immediately answers that

**[00:45:37.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000769)  
issue, I'd like to ask

**[00:45:40.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000770)  
the Senate President kung meron kayong gustong ibigay

**[00:45:41.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000771)  
na opening remarks and actually as our

**[00:45:44.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000772)  
official present here, you'll have first

**[00:45:46.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000773)  
crack at the questions to the Department.

**[00:45:47.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000774)  
Just very short, Madam Chairperson.

**[00:45:50.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000775)

**[00:45:51.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000776)  
Talagang

**[00:45:52.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000777)  
pumapasok tayo sa

**[00:45:55.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000778)  
tawag ko nga doon,

**[00:45:57.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000779)  
triple whammy

**[00:45:58.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000780)  
sa ating bansa,

**[00:46:01.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000781)  
sa ating ekonomiya.

**[00:46:02.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000782)  
In fact, because of that triple whammy,

**[00:46:05.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000783)  
the Senate created the RISE Committee

**[00:46:07.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000784)  
to address the economic

**[00:46:09.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000785)  
slowdown of our country.

**[00:46:12.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000786)  
So, in triple whammy will be

**[00:46:13.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000787)  
the Iran conflict.

**[00:46:15.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000788)  
Number two will be

**[00:46:17.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000789)  
the rise in oil prices

**[00:46:19.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000790)  
and may spillover effect

**[00:46:21.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000791)  
dyan tulad ng inflation.

**[00:46:23.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000792)  
And number three, what we are

**[00:46:25.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000793)  
now experiencing and we

**[00:46:27.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000794)  
will be experiencing,

**[00:46:28.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000795)  
throughout the next few months,

**[00:46:29.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000796)  
will be El Nino.

**[00:46:31.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000797)  
And because of this effect,

**[00:46:36.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000798)  
ang kinakabahan ako, baka hindi natin

**[00:46:40.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000799)  
maabot, hindi natin maabot

**[00:46:43.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000800)  
yung ating target pagdating sa

**[00:46:45.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000801)  
poverty reduction.

**[00:46:47.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000802)  
And we also need to make sure

**[00:46:49.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000803)  
that there are enough social

**[00:46:51.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000804)  
safety nets for inflation.

**[00:46:53.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000805)  
Yung gera sa Iran, on and off eh.

**[00:46:55.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000806)  
Minsan bumababa ang presyo ng langis,

**[00:46:57.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000807)  
Minsan hindi.

**[00:46:58.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000808)  
But every time nang pa-fluctuate yan,

**[00:47:00.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000809)  
tama niyan ang inflation.

**[00:47:01.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000810)  
If you look at inflation, you also look at

**[00:47:03.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000811)  
the price of utilities, the price of oil,

**[00:47:07.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000812)  
and the price of food.

**[00:47:08.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000813)  
Yung food, doon ako nag-worry.

**[00:47:10.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000814)  
That's why we need the social safety nets

**[00:47:12.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000815)  
from the SWD to make sure

**[00:47:14.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000816)  
that our constituents have food to eat,

**[00:47:17.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000817)  
their children go to school,

**[00:47:19.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000818)  
hindi nakakain yung kanilang

**[00:47:22.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000819)  
disposable income

**[00:47:24.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000820)  
ng inflation.

**[00:47:27.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000821)  
At,

**[00:47:28.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000822)  
nakakapagtrabaho sila ng maayos.

**[00:47:31.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000823)  
So, we will hear from the department

**[00:47:32.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000824)  
what are the interventions on those

**[00:47:35.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000825)  
specific aspects.

**[00:47:37.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000826)  
And maraming salamat,

**[00:47:40.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000828)
