**[00:47:43.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000829)  
So,

**[00:47:44.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000830)  
you have just framed the

**[00:47:46.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000831)  
matter of social protection

**[00:47:48.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000832)  
also as an economic issue.

**[00:47:50.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000833)  
So, salamat po para doon.

**[00:47:52.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000834)  
So, yes, Secrex, if you'd like to

**[00:47:54.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000835)  
first of all respond to the

**[00:47:56.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000836)  
issues raised about the,

**[00:47:58.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000837)  
the fake

**[00:47:58.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000838)  
persons with disability IDs

**[00:48:01.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000839)  
and then we'll open the first round

**[00:48:03.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000840)  
of questioning with the SP.

**[00:48:05.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000841)  
Please proceed, sir.

**[00:48:06.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000842)  
Madam Chair, as pointed out by

**[00:48:08.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000843)  
Senator Tulfo, in his committee,

**[00:48:10.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000844)  
we have tackled this issue.

**[00:48:11.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000845)  
But let me state it for the record.

**[00:48:13.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000846)  
After the, at that time, when we

**[00:48:15.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000847)  
found out, nung napabalita

**[00:48:18.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000848)  
itong napaglaganap ng fake ID,

**[00:48:20.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000849)  
the department immediately went to work

**[00:48:21.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000850)  
and proposed a unified system.

**[00:48:24.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000851)  
The unified ID system. It's a digital

**[00:48:25.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000852)  
format, wala ng physical ID,

**[00:48:28.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000853)  
the beauty of this system, ready na ho siya.

**[00:48:30.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000854)  
In fact, it's the same system we are about

**[00:48:31.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000855)  
to use for solo parents also, or we're using

**[00:48:34.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000856)  
for solo parents. Ang mangyayari lang ho,

**[00:48:36.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000857)  
wala ng ID-ID, it's a digital ID.

**[00:48:37.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000858)  
You get a QR code, pag ni-scan ho ng

**[00:48:39.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000859)  
establishment, you'll know if it's a real

**[00:48:42.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000860)  
ID, kasi nasa database yung tao

**[00:48:44.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000861)  
or wala. We were about to roll it out,

**[00:48:46.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000862)  
but the biggest problem is, when we look at the

**[00:48:47.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000863)  
IRR of the PWD discounts

**[00:48:49.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000864)  
law, man parang the QR

**[00:48:52.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000865)  
code ng IBP. Ganun lang. Yeah.

**[00:48:54.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000866)  
Scan lang ng establishment,

**[00:48:56.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000867)  
no new requirements, cellphone lang nila,

**[00:48:58.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000868)  
pwede na. Naka-piggyback rin siya

**[00:49:00.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000869)  
sa e-gov. So when we were about

**[00:49:02.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000870)  
to roll it out, we looked at the IRR

**[00:49:04.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000871)  
of the PWD discount law, at

**[00:49:06.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000872)  
because of the devolution ng social welfare,

**[00:49:08.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000873)  
walang role ang DSWD

**[00:49:10.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000874)  
doon. We weren't, ang issuing

**[00:49:12.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000875)  
ID is, nang issuing is LGU.

**[00:49:15.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000876)  
LGU siya.

**[00:49:16.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000877)  
So that's why now we have 1,000, tama ho yung

**[00:49:18.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000878)  
sa RestoPH. In fact, I met them also.

**[00:49:20.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000879)  
1,500 distinct

**[00:49:22.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000880)  
designs of IDs, 1,500

**[00:49:24.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000881)  
issuing agents, or LGUs,

**[00:49:26.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000882)  
and 1,500

**[00:49:28.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000883)  
signatures ng mga

**[00:49:30.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000884)  
iba't-ibang nag-i-issue.

**[00:49:32.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000885)  
Wala ho kami doon. So meaning, our

**[00:49:34.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000886)  
solution was ready, but the problem

**[00:49:36.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000887)  
is we had to work on the reform

**[00:49:38.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000888)  
muna nung

**[00:49:39.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000889)  
what the setup, the current status quo.

**[00:49:43.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000890)  
Ang nakipagpulong

**[00:49:44.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000891)  
ho kami sa BIR to try to

**[00:49:46.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000892)  
amend the revenue code, the revenue regulation

**[00:49:48.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000893)  
code, para ma-encourage yung mga

**[00:49:50.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000894)  
merchants to use our ID

**[00:49:52.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000895)  
kasi clearly, mahirap na bawiin yung

**[00:49:54.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000896)  
mga ID na manual na nasa labas.

**[00:49:56.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000897)  
So what will be their incentive? We promised

**[00:49:58.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000898)  
them na pag ito ang gamitin nyo,

**[00:50:00.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000899)  
makakasigurado kayo na malalaman nyo

**[00:50:02.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000900)  
kung legit or illegitimate yung ID

**[00:50:04.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000901)  
because nga nakapatong sa database.

**[00:50:07.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000902)  
So it took time

**[00:50:08.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000903)  
because we have to amend the implementing rules

**[00:50:10.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000904)  
and regulation ng PWD

**[00:50:12.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000905)  
ID discounts law, pero

**[00:50:14.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000906)  
we're almost done. Anytime in the next

**[00:50:16.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000907)  
two weeks, we will sign with DOF

**[00:50:18.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000908)  
and DOH. Now, just

**[00:50:20.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000909)  
one step backwards. We identified

**[00:50:22.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000910)  
three points of fraud

**[00:50:24.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000911)  
kung saan nangyayari ho yung

**[00:50:26.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000912)  
panluloko. Una,

**[00:50:28.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000913)  
tulad ng pinoint out ni

**[00:50:29.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000914)  
Sen. Erwin at ni Sen. Rafi kanina,

**[00:50:32.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000915)  
noon, maluwag yung

**[00:50:34.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000916)  
implementation kung sinong doktor

**[00:50:35.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000917)  
ang pwedeng mag-issue ng alin.

**[00:50:37.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000918)  
Sometimes, you would see a general practitioner

**[00:50:39.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000919)  
issuing what a dev

**[00:50:41.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000920)  
ped should be issuing, for instance.

**[00:50:44.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000921)  
So ang ginawa namin doon, in the interim,

**[00:50:45.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000922)  
kinausap namin yung DOH para

**[00:50:47.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000923)  
i-tighten nila. Then, Secretary

**[00:50:49.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000924)  
Herbosa issued a AO

**[00:50:51.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000925)  
na dinefine na mabuti.

**[00:50:53.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000926)  
Aling doktor lang ang pwede mag-issue?

**[00:50:56.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000927)  
Mag-issue ng aling certification.

**[00:50:58.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000928)  
So hindi ibig sabihin, pag orthopedic ka,

**[00:50:59.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000929)  
pwede ka na rin mag-issue ng learning

**[00:51:01.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000930)  
disability na certification.

**[00:51:04.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000931)  
So yun, na-tighten na ho yun. That's the

**[00:51:05.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000932)  
first part. Yung very loose

**[00:51:07.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000933)  
yung certification process, which

**[00:51:09.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000934)  
is yun yung requirement na dinadala

**[00:51:11.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000935)  
sa LGU to get the ID.

**[00:51:13.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000936)  
Yung pangalawa, may mga

**[00:51:15.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000937)  
pagkakataon talaga na may kaibigan sa

**[00:51:17.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000938)  
loob ng mga pidaw office ng

**[00:51:19.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000939)  
LGU at nag-iissue na lang ng ID

**[00:51:21.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000940)  
kahit kulang ang papel. That one, we

**[00:51:23.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000941)  
worked with the DILG to make sure na talaga

**[00:51:26.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000942)  
down sa mga LGU. Kasi

**[00:51:28.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000943)  
that's an ethical violation, administrative

**[00:51:29.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000944)  
violation yun. It's a matter of the LGUs

**[00:51:31.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000945)  
enforcing it. And pangatlo,

**[00:51:34.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000946)  
yung simple na lang, pupunta sila sa recto,

**[00:51:36.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000947)  
magpapagwa sila ng fake ID.

**[00:51:38.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000948)  
So that one, masusulusyonan with the unified

**[00:51:39.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000949)  
ID. So sa madaling salita ho, ready

**[00:51:42.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000950)  
na yung system. In fact, habang

**[00:51:43.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000951)  
nagaantay kami dun sa revenue code,

**[00:51:45.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000952)  
almost 80% ng ating

**[00:51:48.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000953)  
mga LGU na-train na namin gamitin yung

**[00:51:49.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000954)  
new system. Pangalawa,

**[00:51:52.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000955)  
yung revenue, yung

**[00:51:53.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000956)  
implementing rules and regulation,

**[00:51:56.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000957)  
we're not asking Senate or the

**[00:51:57.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000958)  
Congress to pass a new law.

**[00:51:59.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000959)  
Ginalaw lang, nakiusap, in-amend lang

**[00:52:01.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000960)  
namin yung IRR to include

**[00:52:03.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000961)  
us, the SWD, as an

**[00:52:05.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000962)  
implement, as a issuing entity.

**[00:52:08.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000963)  
Pinakamabilis,

**[00:52:09.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000964)  
next two weeks, we're about to sign with

**[00:52:11.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000965)  
DOH and DOF.

**[00:52:13.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000966)  
Pag na-implement na yun, suddenly may

**[00:52:15.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000967)  
personality na ho kami mag-issue ng ID, then

**[00:52:17.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000968)  
our unified system can be used already.

**[00:52:20.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000969)  
Pangatlo po,

**[00:52:23.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000970)  
in our

**[00:52:23.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000971)  
briefing, Madam Chair, na kwento namin,

**[00:52:26.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000972)  
ang magiging puhuna namin dito

**[00:52:27.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000973)  
will be giving the smaller LGUs

**[00:52:30.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000974)  
binigyan namin ng software.

**[00:52:32.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000975)  
Sasabihin nila, wala naman kaming hardware.

**[00:52:34.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000976)  
So kaya nga ho, yung budget

**[00:52:35.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000977)  
cut sa NCDA budget,

**[00:52:37.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000978)  
yun ho sana yung ipangbibili namin ng

**[00:52:39.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000979)  
hardware na ibibigay sa mga pidaw

**[00:52:41.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000980)  
or people with disability offices

**[00:52:43.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000981)  
para magamit nila yung app or

**[00:52:45.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000982)  
yung software na ginawa namin.

**[00:52:47.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000983)  
Now, Madam Chair, kanina binanggit ho nyo na

**[00:52:49.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000984)  
digitalization should not be a hindrance

**[00:52:51.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000985)  
to excluding people. Tama ho kayo doon.

**[00:52:54.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000986)  
That's why, itong system na to, may

**[00:52:55.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000987)  
option siya to print. So meaning,

**[00:52:57.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000988)  
the LGUs can print the ID as their service

**[00:52:59.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000989)  
pero ito na ang gamitin nila, huwag na yung

**[00:53:01.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000990)  
sarili nilang ID. So, yun ho.

**[00:53:04.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000991)  
Although, Secrex, I raised that in

**[00:53:05.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000992)  
relation sa four P's, families.

**[00:53:07.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000993)  
Pero kung the same principles sa mga

**[00:53:09.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000994)  
persons with disabilities, i-apply sa kanila,

**[00:53:11.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000995)  
makakatulong po yun.

**[00:53:12.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000995-split-2)  
Yes, Madam Chair, I believe

**[00:53:13.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000996)  
that technology shouldn't be an excluding factor

**[00:53:15.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000997)  
for any social protection program.

**[00:53:18.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000998)  
So yun ho yung una naming dinulog

**[00:53:19.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000999)  
na sinulat namin sa committee na sana ma-restore

**[00:53:21.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001000)  
yung maliit lang naman na pondo sa NCDA

**[00:53:23.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001001)  
para mabili yung computers,

**[00:53:25.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001002)  
at mabigay yung hardware. Kasi

**[00:53:27.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001003)  
sasabihin ng mga LGU, mayroon nga kayong software,

**[00:53:30.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001004)  
may revenue code kayo,

**[00:53:31.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001005)  
wala naman kaming computer. So pag may magpapaprint,

**[00:53:34.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001006)  
paano namin ipiprint?

**[00:53:35.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001007)  
So there are three ingredients. Software is ready

**[00:53:37.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001008)  
with a key database behind it.

**[00:53:40.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001009)  
Ang nagpapopulate naman ho ng database,

**[00:53:42.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001010)  
yung mga pidaw office rin eh. Hindi naman

**[00:53:43.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001011)  
namin kinuha yung power na yun.

**[00:53:45.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001012)  
Pangalawa, ready na rin ho yung

**[00:53:47.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001013)  
IRR na amendment.

**[00:53:50.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001014)  
Anytime magsasign na ho kami,

**[00:53:52.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001015)  
ito na lang ho hardware ang kailangan ho namin.

**[00:53:54.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001016)  
We take cognizance

**[00:53:55.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001017)  
of the issue, kinausap rin po namin yung

**[00:53:57.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001018)  
RestoPH. Nag-meet po kami,

**[00:53:59.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001019)  
together with the PWD groups, and we agree

**[00:54:01.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001020)  
with the observation of

**[00:54:03.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001021)  
Senator Erwin and Senator Raffi na kailangan

**[00:54:05.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001022)  
na talaga actionan ho itong festering

**[00:54:07.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001023)  
na problema.

**[00:54:09.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001024)  
Salamat po, Secrex. Before we

**[00:54:11.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001025)  
begin the first round of

**[00:54:13.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001026)  
questions with the Senate President,

**[00:54:15.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001027)  
baka mas maiging ngayon magpresenta

**[00:54:17.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001028)  
na rin, kahit maikli,

**[00:58:50.480] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001164)  
Secretary

**[00:58:50.940] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001165)  
Gatchalian. Pursuant to

**[00:58:53.500] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001166)  
the issuance of August

**[00:58:55.720] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001167)  
14 by the President

**[00:58:57.660] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001168)  
Ferdinand Bongbong Marcos Jr.,

**[00:58:59.560] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001169)  
Executive Order No. 123,

**[00:59:01.860] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001170)  
NAPC, together with PCUP,

**[00:59:04.240] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001171)  
the two institutions are a part

**[00:59:05.600] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001172)  
of the Office of the President. Originally,

**[00:59:07.700] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001173)  
yes, I agree that

**[00:59:09.120] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001174)  
under Republic Act 8425,

**[00:59:11.780] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001175)  
actually signed into law by

**[00:59:13.680] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001176)  
President Ramos in December 11 of

**[00:59:15.480] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001177)  
1997, with NAPC

**[00:59:17.660] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001178)  
as the lead agency

**[00:59:19.340] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001179)  
on matters pertaining to poverty reduction,

**[00:59:22.460] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001180)  
we main task

**[00:59:23.700] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001181)  
on policy reform,

**[00:59:25.720] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001182)  
recovery, and in accordance with social reform

**[00:59:27.420] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001183)  
agenda. And

**[00:59:28.900] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001184)  
it was only in 2019

**[00:59:31.660] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001185)  
that NAPC was placed

**[00:59:33.460] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001186)  
under the administrative supervision

**[00:59:34.760] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001187)  
of the DSWD.

**[00:59:37.560] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001188)  
And hence, in

**[00:59:39.220] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001189)  
the budget preparations and all,

**[00:59:41.280] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001190)  
we were under the

**[00:59:43.220] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001191)  
DSWD. Last August

**[00:59:45.480] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001192)  
14, the President

**[00:59:46.520] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001193)  
finally decided, also because of

**[00:59:49.580] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001194)  
the task pursuant to Republic Act

**[00:59:51.520] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001195)  
11291, or the

**[00:59:53.300] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001196)  
Magna Carta of the Poor, that

**[00:59:55.420] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001197)  
mandates all local government units

**[00:59:57.480] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001198)  
to formulate

**[00:59:59.240] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001199)  
their

**[01:00:00.080] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001201)  
their provincial, city, and municipal poverty reduction action plan.

**[01:00:06.040] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001202)  
And in that law, as a result of, actually, this was anchored on a 1965 UN Convention.

**[01:00:13.200] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001203)  
The Philippines only passed a law in 2019.

**[01:00:21.080] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001204)  
And the Magna Carta of the Poor that demands a local poverty reduction action plan

**[01:00:27.980] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001205)  
to all local government institutions was only implemented in 2023.

**[01:00:34.940] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001206)  
Imagine and focus on the five fundamental rights of the poor.

**[01:00:40.200] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001207)  
The right to adequate food, the right to the highest attainable standards of health,

**[01:00:45.200] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001208)  
the right to adequate housing, the right to highest attainable standards of health,

**[01:00:54.800] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001209)  
and including the decent work.

**[01:00:59.420] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001210)  
In other words, these five fundamental rights are now in place in the Magna Carta of the Poor

**[01:01:05.920] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001211)  
Local Poverty Reduction Action Plan with a Joint Memorandum Circular 01 Series of 2023

**[01:01:11.140] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001212)  
with DSWD, with then NEDA, now DEPDev, with PSA because of the Community-Based Monitoring System Law.

**[01:01:21.100] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001213)  
And of course, in that context, in the...

**[01:01:24.800] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001214)  
In the en banc meeting presided over by the President in June 30 of 2023,

**[01:01:32.460] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001215)  
the target or the marching order to reducing the poverty incidence to 8.8

**[01:01:36.820] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001216)  
was articulated in that 2023 en banc meeting.

**[01:01:40.540] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001217)  
In the 2024 meeting, also, with the support of both houses, especially the Senate,

**[01:01:47.360] NAPC Undersecretary Esnaen Catong** (asr-001218)  
it was the Chairperson, Risa Hontiveros, and also with the support of Senator Imee Marcos,

**[01:45:56.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002423)  
Oti po yung mga commissioners. Lahat nag-aasikaso dun sa paghahandang iyon.

**[01:46:00.340] NCSC Acting Executive Director Ana Marie C. Calapit** (asr-002424)  
Hindi naman po.

**[01:46:03.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002425)  
So, sana yung mga commissioners na nandirito ay dumalo rin ngayong hapon sa ating hearing.

**[01:46:10.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002426)  
Pakipaabot po yung feedback po ng chair.

**[01:46:13.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002427)  
And aasahan po namin pati si chair at yung mga commissioners ay kasama din po natin sa budget plenary.

**[01:46:21.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002428)  
Yun po ang custom dito po sa mga budget hearings.

**[01:46:24.720] NCSC Acting Executive Director Ana Marie C. Calapit** (asr-002429)  
Okay po, Madam Chair.

**[01:46:25.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002430)  
Salamat.

**[01:46:26.580] NCSC Acting Executive Director Ana Marie C. Calapit** (asr-002431)  
Okay po.

**[01:46:27.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002432)  
Salamat, ED.

**[01:46:27.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002433)  
Anna Marie.

**[01:46:28.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002434)  
Please proceed, Senator Erwin.

**[01:46:29.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002435)  
Salamat.

**[01:46:30.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002436)  
Kaya wala na sila dito, Madam Chair.

**[01:46:32.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002437)  
Pinaubayan nila sa DSWD.

**[01:46:34.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002438)  
Nandiyan naman na DSWD.

**[01:46:36.820] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002439)  
If you will not show up your leaders, yung chairman po ninyo at commissioner,

**[01:46:40.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002440)

**[01:46:44.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002441)  
Pero tama po yun, ma'am.

**[01:46:45.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002442)  
Barang nakaka-insulto.

**[01:46:47.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002443)  
Sabihin nyo na lang ho sa inyong chairman at saka inyong mga commissioners.

**[01:46:51.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002444)  
Ano po?

**[01:46:52.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002445)  
Thank you.

**[01:46:52.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002446)  
Salamat, Senator Erwin.

**[01:46:54.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002447)  
Actually, tingin ko.

**[01:46:55.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002448)  
So, kaya naman nung dalawang ahensya, department at saka commission,

**[01:47:00.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002449)  
i-resolve ito.

**[01:47:01.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002450)  
Alang-alang sa kabutihan ng mga senior citizens natin.

**[01:47:04.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002451)  
At yung autonomy din nung agency na itinayo para sa kanila.

**[01:47:08.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002452)  
Salamat.

**[01:47:08.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002453)  
Please proceed, Senator Erwin.

**[01:47:09.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002454)  
Yes.

**[01:47:10.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002455)  
Last two questions.

**[01:47:12.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002456)  
Let's go to sustainable livelihood program.

**[01:47:15.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002457)  
Binawasan po ba itong SLP ninyo, Secrex?

**[01:47:20.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002458)  
Madam Chair.

**[01:47:21.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002459)  
Madam Chair, it was restored to the NEP level.

**[01:47:24.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002460)  
Kasi yung...

**[01:47:25.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002461)  
Nagdagdagan po, nanggaling ho yun during the amendment period.

**[01:47:29.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002462)  
How much is this po?

**[01:47:30.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002463)  
Ayun, tanggal 1 billion.

**[01:47:32.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002464)  
1 billion, no?

**[01:47:33.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002465)  
And 1B, no?

**[01:47:35.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002466)  
Ang...

**[01:47:35.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002467)  
From the original na 6B, naging 5B na lang ho siya.

**[01:47:41.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002468)  
So, that's why we also put it in our tier 2 na sana dagdagan ho beyond even that level.

**[01:47:46.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002469)  
Kasi when I was a secretary, problema na ho talaga ito sa dami po ng kailangan sa mga regional offices po na ninyo.

**[01:47:54.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002470)  
Pati po yung...

**[01:47:55.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002471)  
Mga LGUs na...

**[01:47:57.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002472)  
Siyempre, gusto po nila na mabigyan na kabuhayan yung constituents nila.

**[01:48:01.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002473)  
Nakikita po nila yun.

**[01:48:03.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002474)  
Pero with limited budget.

**[01:48:04.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002475)  
So, you're saying that you're requesting na probably another 1B para sa sustainable livelihood?

**[01:48:09.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002476)  
Actually, Madam Chair, during our tier 2, pinaabot ho namin yung request namin na karagdagang 3.5B para kukam na close to 10 billion.

**[01:48:17.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002477)  
Tama ho kayo.

**[01:48:19.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002478)  
Madam Chair, the good senator knows this very well.

**[01:48:22.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002479)  
Itong programa na ito, nakakatulong ito in the long run.

**[01:48:25.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002480)  
Kasi halimbawa yung umaalis sa 4Bs, doon natin pinapasok para hindi nababalik sa kahirapan.

**[01:48:30.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002481)  
And it's teaching them how to fish.

**[01:48:32.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002482)  
Tama po yun, Madam Chair.

**[01:48:34.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002483)  
Because parang grant po ito kasi, Madam Chair, itong SLP.

**[01:48:39.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002484)  
Hindi kagayang sa DTI, binabayaran sila.

**[01:48:42.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002485)  
Pero ito talagang ibibigay po ng DSWD para pangkabuhayan.

**[01:48:46.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002486)  
Well, anyway, moving to another topic.

**[01:48:49.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002487)  
Ito, medyo sensitive po ito at saka napapanahon.

**[01:48:53.560] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002488)  
Last year po, kami po...

**[01:48:55.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002489)  
Ni SP, tinaasan po namin when he was the Finance Chair, yung inyo pong Assistance for Individuals in Crisis.

**[01:49:05.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002490)  
Wala pa pong gera noon.

**[01:49:07.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002491)  
Wala pa pong mga sakuna, kung ano man po yan, dilubyo.

**[01:49:12.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002492)  
Pero we anticipated na baka may mangyari kasi medyo nagkakagirian na po yung Amerika at Iran.

**[01:49:19.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002493)  
Tama nga po yung naging desisyon na dagdagan po kayo.

**[01:49:21.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002494)  
Umabot po tayo ng 63 something billion.

**[01:49:24.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002495)  
Di tama nga.

**[01:49:25.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002496)  
Ang presidente, kayong unang inilagay sa front line to address the problems.

**[01:49:29.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002497)  
Pwede po ba ng mga ating mga transport, pambilin ng mga gasolina, yung mga OFWs natin, tumulong din po kayo.

**[01:49:38.840] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002498)  
So talagang malaki po na gastos po ninyo doon.

**[01:49:42.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002499)  
Pero nagtataka lang po ako, binawasan po sa NEP ninyo.

**[01:49:47.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002500)  
Pero dinagdagan naman na umabot na po sa 30 something billion.

**[01:49:50.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002501)  
Well, that is sufficient enough.

**[01:49:53.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002502)  
We're anticipating right now.

**[01:49:55.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002503)  
Paparating po itong El Nino.

**[01:49:57.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002504)  
Tapos ito pong gyera sa Iran, di pa po tapos.

**[01:50:00.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002505)  
Hindi natin alam kung hanggang kailan po ito.

**[01:50:03.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002506)  
Baka ikang umabot na rito.

**[01:50:05.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002507)  
Pati nga po yung Yemen, Somalia, Saudi Arabia, mga Houthis.

**[01:50:11.860] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002508)  
Kung saan saan na ho nag-ikang umugat itong problema nito.

**[01:50:15.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002509)  
Meron tayong yun.

**[01:50:16.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002510)  
Yung El Nino, tapos hindi pa po natin alam kung ano talagang epekto nito.

**[01:50:23.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002511)  
So, papano ito?

**[01:50:25.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002512)  
What are you planning?

**[01:50:26.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002513)  
Is it enough?

**[01:50:27.800] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002514)  
Is it enough na dinagdagan po sa gab?

**[01:50:30.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002515)  
Unfortunately, una po, Madam Chair,

**[01:50:32.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002516)  
gusto kong magpasalamat kay Senator Erwin,

**[01:50:35.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002517)  
kay Senator Sherwin dahil binalik ko yan na dinagdagan.

**[01:50:38.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002518)  
Yung dinagdag ho naging standby fund natin.

**[01:50:40.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002519)  
Nung kasagsagan nito, at hindi ko naman pwede sabihin natapos na,

**[01:50:43.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002520)  
nung sa transport sector alone,

**[01:50:45.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002521)  
close to 10 billion in one month ang ginamit natin para sa mga transport workers natin.

**[01:50:51.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002522)  
Doon natin kinuha yun.

**[01:50:53.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002523)  
It became our lifeline.

**[01:50:55.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002524)  
So, kaya naka-respond tayo kaagad.

**[01:50:56.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002525)  
In fact, nagpa-quick survey ho ang SWS tulad na nabanggit ko kanina.

**[01:51:01.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002526)  
96% of the respondents doon sa catchment said,

**[01:51:04.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002527)  
ginamit nila yung pera para magpatuloy ng hanap buhay.

**[01:51:07.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002528)  
80% naman ang sinabi, ginamit nila yun para makabili ng pagkain nung nangangailangan sila.

**[01:51:13.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002529)  
So, ngayon nangangamba ho kami sa pagbawas na meron nga triple whammy na ina-anticipate,

**[01:51:18.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002530)  
pero nabawasan naman ho kami.

**[01:51:19.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002531)  
Kaya nga ho, maraming salamat sa tanong na dumudulog kami ngayon ulit

**[01:51:22.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002532)  
sa Komitee at sa Senado na sana ibalik man lang doon sa level ng GAAP last year.

**[01:51:30.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002533)  
Kahit ngayon, very tight na eh.

**[01:51:33.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002534)  
Pero kung talagang tight fiscal space, maibalik man lang doon.

**[01:51:36.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002535)  
Palagay ko, mas kakayanin natin ng konti po.

**[01:51:39.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002536)  
Will that sustain isa ka sakali?

**[01:51:42.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002537)  
I mean, ang pinag-usapan lang kasi natin, in-anticipate natin po yung El Niño.

**[01:51:46.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002538)  
Pero paano kung may mga darating pa ng mga dilubyok at baka magkakaroon pa po ng gera,

**[01:51:51.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002539)  
hindi ho natin alam.

**[01:51:52.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002540)  
Will that be sufficient na ikanga para, kasi kayo po laging pag may mga ganito sitwasyon,

**[01:51:59.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002541)  
DSWD, nandoon ho kayo sa front line.

**[01:52:02.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002542)  
May problema po ang mga magsasakan natin, DSWD, natulong din po.

**[01:52:06.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002543)  
Mangingis na, DSWD pa rin ho.

**[01:52:08.840] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002544)  
So, ano ho, what is your take on this?

**[01:52:14.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002545)  
Sabi ko ho ito sa House, nung pinatawag ho kami during the budget season,

**[01:52:17.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002546)  
nangangamba rin ho kami.

**[01:52:20.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002547)  
Nangangamba kami na yung natira na,

**[01:52:22.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002548)  
eh maaaring hindi sasapat, kaya dumudulog ho kami na sana ibalik man lang,

**[01:52:26.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002549)  
man lang, to the minimum sa gaal level.

**[01:52:30.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002550)  
Madam Chair, tama ho kayo na kulang kung titignan.

**[01:52:34.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002551)  
Pero gusto ko rin ho i-assure yung committee na kung mabalik ho yun,

**[01:52:37.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002552)  
kaya naman namin i-balance siya with the quick response.

**[01:52:42.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002553)  
Kasi kanina nabanggit na rin ho yung quick response.

**[01:52:44.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002554)  
Dahil the quick response, kami ho ang pinaka, DSWD po ang pinakamalaking user po noon.

**[01:52:49.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002555)  
So, pinagsasabay ho namin gamitin yan.

**[01:52:51.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002556)  
Pero admittedly, kahit tignan nyo yung quick response,

**[01:52:53.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002557)  
by this time, medyo konti na rin ho yung balance ng Ndream Fund.

**[01:52:58.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002558)  
Meron pa, pero konti na.

**[01:53:00.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002559)  
Now, Madam Chair, there was a year na binibigyan kami ng Office of,

**[01:53:04.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002560)  
which is, hindi naman dapat,

**[01:53:06.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002561)  
but binibigyan kami rin ng contingency fund galing sa Office of the President to augment.

**[01:53:11.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002562)  
Pero alam ko po yung sinasabi nyo na paano pag hindi kayo bigyan,

**[01:53:15.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002563)  
eh di mabibitin ho.

**[01:53:16.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002564)  
And kung pwede ho, dagdagan pa beyond the gaal level because of the anticipated,

**[01:53:21.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002565)  
inflationary effects na tuloy-tuloy.

**[01:53:24.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002566)  
Yung El Nino na not just the farmers, but the prices of goods and food actually,

**[01:53:30.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002567)  
baka tumaas rin.

**[01:53:32.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002568)  
And then, meron pa ho yung sunod-sunod na mga regular ho na mga pangangailangan,

**[01:53:36.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002569)  
talagang kukulangin po.

**[01:53:38.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002570)  
Alright.

**[01:53:39.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002571)  
Yung QRF ho na nabanggit ho ninyo last year,

**[01:53:43.040] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002572)  
nung nagkagulo na nga po, nagkagero na nga.

**[01:53:45.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002573)  
I called my good friend, Yuseca Dayan Cahipe.

**[01:53:50.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002574)  
Sabi niya, meron pa naman.

**[01:53:51.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002575)  
Sen, may budget pa.

**[01:53:53.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002576)  
Pero kailangan lang i-juggle siguro yung Secretary Rex Gatchal yan.

**[01:53:57.200] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002577)  
Kumusta naman po itong QRF ninyo?

**[01:53:59.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002578)  
Nabawasan ba?

**[01:54:00.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002579)  
Ninagdagan ba?

**[01:54:02.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002580)  
Kailangan ba medyo malaki-laki?

**[01:54:04.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002581)  
Just in case po.

**[01:54:05.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002582)  
Just in case.

**[01:54:05.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002583)  
Hindi naman natin ikang pinagdarasal na sana dumating lahat itong problema.

**[01:54:11.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002584)  
Pero will that be sufficient po itong QRF?

**[01:54:14.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002585)  
Sen, Madam Chair.

**[01:54:16.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002586)  
Una po, nagpapasalamatin kami sa inyo.

**[01:54:18.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002587)  
Dahil yung standby fund na seed capital ng QRF, nilakihan ho nyo last time.

**[01:54:23.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002588)  
Umabot na ho ng 3 billion yun.

**[01:54:25.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002589)  
Crucial ho kasi yun dahil yung replenishment mechanism takes around,

**[01:54:30.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002590)  
magbilis na ho ngayon.

**[01:54:31.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002591)  
But syempre meron hong documentation.

**[01:54:33.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002592)  
Umabot ng 2 weeks.

**[01:54:35.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002593)  
So in that 2 weeks time, maraming pwedeng mangyari.

**[01:54:38.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002594)  
Nag-aantay yung beneficiaryo.

**[01:54:40.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002595)  
Pero kaya nga ho, natuwa kami na sa house, ibinalik sa 3 billion.

**[01:54:45.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002596)  
From 1.5, ibinalik sa 2.1.

**[01:54:48.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002597)  
1.5, 1.25, ibinalik ulit sa 3.

**[01:54:52.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002598)  
Kasi that gives us the leg room na yung term nyo, mabalans eh.

**[01:54:56.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002599)  
So habang humihingi kami ng replenishment, tuloy-tuloy pa rin activity sa baba

**[01:55:00.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002600)  
sa pagbayad ng emergency cash transfer.

**[01:55:02.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002601)

bacta ng

4Ps

**[02:06:44.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002873)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:06:45.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002874)  
Mt. Sen goes

**[02:06:46.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002875)  
The Sen. is correct

**[02:06:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002876)  
Nabawasan po pero

**[02:06:48.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002877)  
just for the record

**[02:06:49.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002878)  
hindi ho dahil

**[02:06:50.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002879)  
yung programa ay

**[02:06:52.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002880)  
Hindi hamaapekto nyo yung program

**[02:06:54.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002881)  
Ang instructo ng ating Pangulo

**[02:06:55.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002882)  
to make sure na reflective yung numero ng four-piece family sa poverty incidents.

**[02:07:00.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002883)  
So this was the poverty incidents pangaho na 15.5%.

**[02:07:05.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002884)  
Hindi pangaho yung 9% kasi kakalabas lang nung 9%.

**[02:07:08.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002885)  
So nung una ho kasi, that number has been pegged for the longest time at 4.4 million families,

**[02:07:14.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002886)  
no matter kung anuman ang naging poverty incidents.

**[02:07:17.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002887)  
So ang direktiba ng ating Pangulo is to make sure that the list is clean.

**[02:07:22.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002888)  
Kasi nung sinimulan to ni Sen Erwin, lumabas lang siya but memorize niya to,

**[02:07:27.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002889)  
sinimulan niya yung cleaning of the list.

**[02:07:30.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002890)  
So tinuloy ho natin nung pumasok tayo but we did it also through proper case management.

**[02:07:36.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002891)  
Ang nangyari ho dito, we hired more nung dumating ho kami na pagbigyan tayo natamihan yung mga case managers natin,

**[02:07:44.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002892)  
yung mga city links natin.

**[02:07:45.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002893)  
They are the lifeline of the program kasi sila yung nagmamonitor kung pwede na i-exit.

**[02:07:50.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002894)  
So ngayon, kapag i-exit,

**[02:07:52.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002895)  
na-exit na tayo ng 1.77 million.

**[02:07:55.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002896)  
That's from 2022 all the way to 2025.

**[02:08:00.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002897)  
Prior to that, ang na-exit lang sa programa was 15,000 families.

**[02:08:04.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002898)  
So napakalayo na ho nung numero.

**[02:08:06.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002899)  
Now, the updates to the program, number one, the case management has been strengthened.

**[02:08:13.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002900)  
More social workers and development workers doing case management.

**[02:08:18.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002901)  
Para yung mga dapat ma-exit, pwede nang ma-exit.

**[02:08:20.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002902)  
Pangalawa,

**[02:08:22.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002903)  
yung in-automate rin ho natin, and it's live now, the system is live.

**[02:08:26.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002904)  
Dati-rati ho kasi, there are 14 systems that do not talk to each other.

**[02:08:31.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002905)  
Kaya matagal yung grants na napukuha.

**[02:08:33.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002906)  
Right now, the new system that we call 4Ps Forward is already live.

**[02:08:37.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002907)  
It will take full effect by December.

**[02:08:40.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002908)  
Anong kakaiba sa system na to?

**[02:08:42.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002909)  
It's fully integrated.

**[02:08:43.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002910)  
So from the time you register as a beneficiary all the way to payout,

**[02:08:46.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002911)  
wala na hong download, encode, upload, wala na hong ganon.

**[02:08:50.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002912)  
So we've armed our 14...

**[02:08:52.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002913)  
15,000 na mga links, case managers, with an iPad, with a tablet, and a laptop.

**[02:09:00.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002914)  
Kasi nga, there's no point in buying a new system if you don't arm them with hardware.

**[02:09:06.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002915)  
So kompleto na ho sila.

**[02:09:08.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002916)  
So the beauty of that is, kung dati-rati nag-aantay ho ang ating mga grantees

**[02:09:13.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002917)  
ng almost 60 days to 75 days to get their grants,

**[02:09:16.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002918)  
in the new system, makukuha na nila yung health grant nila at rise subsidy nila

**[02:09:21.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002919)  
within the same month, or 45 days, within 45 days.

**[02:09:25.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002920)  
Now, also, meron ho itong collateral benefits sa DepEd.

**[02:09:29.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002921)  
The new system is now self-contained.

**[02:09:31.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002922)  
Kasi dati, kinukomplain ng DepEd na yung mga guro nila ang gumagawa ng compliance,

**[02:09:36.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002923)  
kaya napaka-volumous nung report doon sa EDUC grant.

**[02:09:39.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002924)  
So nababawasan yung oras nila magturo.

**[02:09:41.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002925)  
So by the end of the year, it will be beneficiaries-driven na.

**[02:09:45.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002926)  
Hindi na kami aasa sa mga guro natin na gumawa ng report,

**[02:09:48.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002927)  
kundi yung report card na mismo ng mga bata,

**[02:09:51.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002928)  
ng ating mga magulang, during the family development session,

**[02:09:55.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002929)  
scan, update the record, you get your education grant.

**[02:09:58.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002930)  
Sa health grant, ganun din.

**[02:09:59.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002931)  
They will have their own booklets na hindi na kami aasa sa mga DOH barangay doctors

**[02:10:04.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002932)  
kasi ang dami na nilang ginagawa.

**[02:10:06.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002933)  
So the system is self-contained and it's more efficient.

**[02:10:09.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002934)  
Now, we are also coming up with a support program for the child-monitored children.

**[02:10:16.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002935)  
Nakita natin sa datos, and alam ko, alam to ng Senate President,

**[02:10:19.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002936)  
pagdating sa comparison ng studies between four-piece and non-four-piece sa basic ed,

**[02:10:26.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002937)  
pantay, near universal.

**[02:10:28.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002938)  
Pagdating sa secondary ed, education,

**[02:10:31.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002939)  
the four-piece beneficiaries does slightly better than non-four-piece beneficiaries,

**[02:10:36.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002940)  
pero sabay rin silang bumabagsak pagdating sa tertiary education.

**[02:10:40.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002941)  
Ang maganda hong nagpapasalamat kami dahil sa new tertiary amendments to the TES,

**[02:10:45.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002942)  
e ngayon talagang categorical na sinabi na four-piece not listed.

**[02:10:49.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002943)  
So talagang, we've already embarked on a massive education campaign to our beneficiaries to avail of this service.

**[02:10:56.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002944)  
Pero during my talks with the beneficiaries, marami sa kanila pa rin ang sinasabi,

**[02:11:00.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002945)  
yung cost of going to school is the problem.

**[02:11:03.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002946)  
Kasi marami sa kanila na sa mga bundok, so they have to, bundok at isla,

**[02:11:07.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002947)  
or geographically isolated and disadvantaged area,

**[02:11:10.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002948)  
luluwa sila sa mga capital or city centers para mag-dorm, para mag-aral.

**[02:11:15.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002949)  
So the program has a distinct feature.

**[02:11:19.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002950)  
It's called the Enhanced Support Service Intervention.

**[02:11:23.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002951)  
Nung dumating ako nandiyan na ho yan, it's enshrined in the guideline.

**[02:11:26.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002952)  
Madati, yung pondo na yun, it's part of the budget already, we're not going to ask for more.

**[02:11:30.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002953)  
Ginagamit lang yan sa a varied myriad of things.

**[02:11:34.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002954)  
May livelihood, okay naman, merong support, other support.

**[02:11:38.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002955)  
Now we've converted it and we're launching it come December.

**[02:11:42.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002956)  
It's called the Tuloy Program, meaning Tuloy Kolehyo, Tuloy Aral at Tuloy Trabaho.

**[02:11:48.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002957)  
So we, consulting with development experts, we will now come up with yung mga monitored children.

**[02:11:56.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002958)  
If they can show that nag-enroll sila, then we will give them a one-time 10,000 grant na 10,000 assistance para mag-tuloy ng kolehyo.

**[02:12:04.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002959)  
Now, succeedingly, if they continue to stay in college, then they can show the attendance through the requirements we'll ask for.

**[02:12:11.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002960)  
Tuloy Aral naman, they get additional 5,000 that they can use together with the allowance they get from TES

**[02:12:17.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002961)  
to support

**[02:12:18.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002963)  
their course of studying.

**[02:12:20.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002964)  
And last, Tuloy Trabaho is when they do graduate out of college, we give them another one-time grant na 10,000 so that they can embark on career,

**[02:12:32.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002965)  
meaning to get the requirements, whatever pre-employment requirements they need.

**[02:12:36.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002966)  
So, in-enhance lang namin yung program to make sure that we lengthen the horizon at matuloy talaga yung pag-aaral nila.

**[02:12:42.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002967)  
Now, I know I've said a lot, but those are the program enhancements, but if you're looking for the impacts, we can flash

**[02:12:48.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002968)  
on the screen the impacts.

**[02:12:51.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002969)  
Kanina ko ba sinabi?

**[02:12:52.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002970)  
Who's the impacts?

**[02:12:58.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002971)  
Akin yung impact.

**[02:13:01.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002972)  
Kanina pala sinabi, hindi mo nalimot.

**[02:13:04.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002973)  
Ito ba yung page 9 nyo sa CREX?

**[02:13:07.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002974)  
Okay, there we are.

**[02:13:07.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002975)  
So, after 18 years, very, very clear na ho yung mga impacts, numerous impact assessments have been done.

**[02:13:14.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002976)  
First things first, the World Bank has already said,

**[02:13:17.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002977)  
na number one, the four-piece program can be attributed to 2.2% poverty reduction.

**[02:13:25.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002978)  
Its cost per, its rate of return is also one of the better ones for the government.

**[02:13:31.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002979)  
Ano saan ba yan?

**[02:13:33.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002980)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:13:34.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002981)  
Hindi ko nga makita

**[02:13:34.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002982)  
Which page?

**[02:13:36.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002983)  
...

**[02:13:37.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002984)  
...

**[02:13:39.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002985)  
Nasaan ito ba?

**[02:13:40.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002986)  
...

**[02:13:42.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002987)  
So, again, the rate of return is one of the better ones.

**[02:13:45.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002988)  
And then,

**[02:13:47.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002989)  
So, these are independent assessments done by so many groups.

**[02:13:51.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002990)  
Also, there's a World Bank study that is already published that says,

**[02:13:55.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002991)  
number one, our four-piece children compared to our non-four-piece children stay in school longer by almost

**[02:14:02.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002992)  
53% of our former youth beneficiaries earn the bachelor's degree versus 31% for non-beneficiaries.

**[02:14:13.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002993)  
So, that means they're staying in school and completing their college education.

**[02:14:17.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002994)  
Then, 44% of those na beneficiaries namin end up with permanent jobs, nearly double the non-four-piece na beneficiaries.

**[02:14:28.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002995)  
25% lang yun, kami 44%.

**[02:14:30.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002996)  
So, meaning nakakapagtrabaho sila, so natutuloy na yung pagputol ng intergenerational poverty.

**[02:14:36.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002997)  
And last, they also cited the 55% of our young women, former young four-piece beneficiaries,

**[02:14:44.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002998)  
achieve permanent employment compared to 33%.

**[02:14:47.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002999)  
So, nakikita na po natin yung fruition ng programa.

**[02:14:52.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003000)  
Although early, pero 18 years later, nasusukat na natin concretely na nabibreak yung intergenerational poverty.

**[02:15:00.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003001)  
Beyond the numbers that I showed kanina, how many thousands graduated, these numbers speak for the program's fruition of breaking intergenerational poverty.

**[02:15:09.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003002)  
Also, in the PSA study, sorry, not the PSA study, the latest FIES of PSA, they cited five things why poverty also went down to 9%.

**[02:15:24.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003003)  
First, in a household, you would get foreign remittance, so yung mga OFW nila nakamag-anak, entrepreneurship, nakapaghanap ng hanap buhay,

**[02:15:35.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003004)  
the controlled prices dahil inflation was low, but one of the reasons they also cited is cash transfers.

**[02:15:43.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003005)  
And a big chunk of that in protecting the households and pulling them out of poverty was cash transfer.

**[02:15:49.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003006)  
And attributable yan to a big chunk sa 4Ps.

**[02:15:54.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003007)  
Thank you for those answers.

**[02:16:01.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003008)  
Actually, itong program na ito, ang isang programa na pag bumaba ba yung budget, natutuwa ko eh.

**[02:16:08.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003009)  
Dahil alam natin na bumaba ba yung bilang ng mahihirap sa ating bansa.

**[02:16:13.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003010)  
Of course, malayo pa tayo with 3.5 million poorest of the poor family.

**[02:16:20.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003011)  
But tutuwa ko dahil 1.7 million ang...

**[02:16:24.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003012)  
na ilabas natin sa poorest of the poor category.

**[02:16:28.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003013)  
Ngayon, my next question is, how do we continue the gains?

**[02:16:35.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003014)  
This is related to what I said earlier, which is El Nino.

**[02:16:39.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003015)  
And also, yung last PSA poverty survey was 2025.

**[02:16:47.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003016)  
Wala pang gera ng era nun.

**[02:16:49.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003017)  
Wala pa tayong El Nino.

**[02:16:51.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003018)  
Wala pa tayong 7.5 inflation.

**[02:16:54.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003019)  
2025, actually, mababa ang inflation natin at that time.

**[02:16:58.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003020)  
Inflation is a, I would call it, is a poverty, it can cause poverty in our country.

**[02:17:09.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003021)  
So, how do we maintain yung pababa na trajectory ng poverty reduction natin?

**[02:17:21.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003022)  
I'll start off with El Nino.

**[02:17:24.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003023)  
And then, I also want to understand anong ginagawa ng department to prepare for this El Nino.

**[02:17:31.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003024)  
Kanina sinabi ni Secretary na they're looking at...

**[02:17:35.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003025)  
Secretary Kiko Laurela, they're looking at...

**[02:17:44.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003026)  
close to 350,000 affected farmers.

**[02:17:52.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003027)  
No?

**[02:17:53.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003028)  
So, medyo...

**[02:17:54.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003029)  
Malaki-laki yun.

**[02:18:00.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003030)  
And, of course, itong affected farmers,

**[02:18:04.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003031)  
mawawalan sila ng trabaho, mawawalan sila ng livelihood.

**[02:18:07.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003032)  
So, how are we preparing for that?

**[02:18:09.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003033)  
Secrex, bago kayo sumagot, gusto lang i-acknowledge ng chair,

**[02:18:13.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003034)  
yung presensya ng aming Finance Committee Chair, Senator JV.

**[02:18:18.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003035)  
Salamat sa pagdalo ninyo.

**[02:18:20.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003036)  
At presensya din ni Senator Bong Go.

**[02:18:23.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003037)  
Salamat kayo, Senator.

**[02:18:24.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003038)  
Thank you, Senator Bong, for joining us.

**[02:18:26.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003039)  
After yung itong first round ni SP, tanongin ko kayo dalawa kung gusto nyo mag-opening remarks.

**[02:18:33.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003040)  
When the...

**[02:18:35.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003041)  
Ang sabi pala ni Secretary Kiko Laurela is 4 million farmers can be affected at the worst case scenario.

**[02:18:42.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003042)  
And we have 10 million workers in the agriculture industry.

**[02:18:47.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003043)  
Dahil nga, iba, drought eh.

**[02:18:48.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003044)  
Wala silang pwedeng pagtaniman.

**[02:18:53.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003045)  
At...

**[02:18:53.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003046)  
Talagang magkakaroon tayo ng widespread unemployment kung at the worst case scenario.

**[02:18:59.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003047)  
So, two questions.

**[02:19:01.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003048)  
One is, how do we prevent El Nino?

**[02:19:04.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003049)  
Or how do we mitigate El Nino in terms of poverty reduction?

**[02:19:08.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003050)  
And how are we addressing El Nino this coming next few months?

**[02:19:12.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003051)  
Madam Sir, bago ko siguro masagot yung El Nino,

**[02:19:15.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003052)  
lagay ko muna sa konteksto nung responde namin sa energy emergency.

**[02:19:19.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003053)  
Kasi yung energy emergency was already detrimental or nanganib na ho sila noon.

**[02:19:23.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003054)  
So, when I say sila, yung mga near poor and poor families ho natin,

**[02:19:27.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003055)  
kasama yung mga 4Ps ho natin doon.

**[02:19:30.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003056)  
We were working closely with the PEDS or one of their fellows,

**[02:19:33.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003057)  
si Professor Toots Albert, in designing the uplift assistance.

**[02:19:37.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003058)  
This comes into play kasi we were looking at the diminished na spending power nila

**[02:19:42.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003059)  
of almost around 1,200 to 1,500 pesos.

**[02:19:45.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003060)  
Doon namin na-draw yung 2,000 pesos monthly na ipinamahagi sa kanila.

**[02:19:50.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003061)  
So, we embarked on a...

**[02:19:52.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003062)  
Tawag namin,

**[02:19:53.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003063)  
This is an EVH, Emergency Scheme, Vertical Expansion or Horizontal Expansion.

**[02:19:58.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003064)  
Ito ho yung rasyonale ng design ng uplift assistance.

**[02:20:01.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003065)  
That's why una, may Group 1 tayo.

**[02:20:03.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003066)  
Nakatanggap ang ating mga 4Ps beneficiaries ng one-time top-up na 2,000 pesos last July or August.

**[02:20:11.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003067)  
The second one was Group 2, meaning yung mga near poor or poor na hindi ho bahagi ng 4Ps

**[02:20:17.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003068)  
dahil they did not meet the conditionalities.

**[02:20:19.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003069)  
There are around 2.5 million households doon.

**[02:20:23.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003070)  
Yun ho.

**[02:20:23.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003071)  
Nakakatanggap yun at patuloy nakakatanggap ng 2,000 pesos since July all the way to December.

**[02:20:28.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003072)  
Sa aming pag-uusap sa uplift committee at sa direktiba ng ating Pangulo,

**[02:20:31.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003073)  
we are playing it close to the ground na kung humaba yung energy emergency,

**[02:20:35.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003074)  
e maaring humaba rin to.

**[02:20:37.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003075)  
So, for now, ang design niya is still December, but we will look at the situation

**[02:20:41.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003076)  
and if we're warranted, sinabi naman ang ating Pangulo that we can extend this.

**[02:20:45.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003077)  
Now, Group 3 is yung low-wage SSS earners.

**[02:20:50.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003078)  
That's 1.5 million families naman.

**[02:20:53.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003079)  
Individuals or household.

**[02:20:54.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003080)  
Yan naman ho, kinonsider namin sila na vulnerable because their monthly salary credits only amounts to 20,000.

**[02:21:01.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003081)  
In fact, later on, in-expand pa namin all the way to mas mababa na 10,000 pesos monthly salary credit.

**[02:21:07.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003082)  
Now, Madam Chair, I spoke about this kasi yung target, etong mga grupo na to,

**[02:21:12.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003083)  
sigurado ko sila rin yung maapektuhan ng Elminio.

**[02:21:15.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003084)  
So, right now, we gave protection to 7.5 million families.

**[02:21:22.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003085)  
A lot of these families will be the same families that will be severely affected by the Elminio drought.

**[02:21:28.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003086)  
Marami, 70% to 85% of our four-piece beneficiaries are farmers.

**[02:21:34.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003087)  
In fact, a big chunk of Group 2, Group 1, are not in the urban areas.

**[02:21:39.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003088)  
They're in the municipalities or rural areas.

**[02:21:42.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003089)  
Nakikita namin yan when we do the payouts.

**[02:21:45.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003090)  
Now, moving forward, I want to show our slide.

**[02:21:49.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003091)  
Meron ba tayong presentation ng El Niño?

**[02:21:51.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003092)  
...

**[02:21:51.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003093)  
So, the El Niño preparations of the department.

**[02:21:56.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003094)  
Ito po, when I got to the department, and alam ko nire-reform rin ito ni Sen. Erwin noon, hindi lang yan natapos.

**[02:22:02.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003095)  
We have a line item called the Disaster Resilience Response Rehabilitation Program, DRRP.

**[02:22:09.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003096)  
That's a 1.1 billion expenditure in our books.

**[02:22:13.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003097)  
Now, nung nadatna namin yan, that's a cash-for-work program na yung work program was very loose

**[02:22:18.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003098)  
in the sense na iba-iba yung pinagagawa sa kanila.

**[02:22:20.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003099)  
So, what we did was,

**[02:22:21.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003100)  
we consulted with the Department of Agriculture, World Food Program,

**[02:22:24.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003101)  
and we revamped it as early as 2023,

**[02:22:27.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003102)  
na isa, dalawa lang ang ginagawa nila,

**[02:22:28.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003103)  
two monolithic programs nationwide.

**[02:22:30.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003104)  
It's called LAWA, which are sweeps yun,

**[02:22:33.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003105)  
yung small water impounding projects.

**[02:22:37.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003106)  
So, we asked the A, where are the springs?

**[02:22:39.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003107)  
Kasi doon namin nilalagay yung mga sweeps natin.

**[02:22:43.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003108)  
And then they would also plant around it.

**[02:22:45.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003109)  
Now, right now, these are found in communities that were targeted initially by the Climate Change Commission,

**[02:22:51.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003110)  
and pag-aasa as drought.

**[02:22:53.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003111)  
Noon pa, as drought, malapit sila sa drought, or any forms of climate change.

**[02:23:00.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003112)  
So, these are the same communities that will be affected or are being affected already by El Nino.

**[02:23:05.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003113)  
So, these sweeps or small water impounding systems na help them irrigate small community farms

**[02:23:12.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003114)  
and at the same time provide income because yung mga naaani nila dyan,

**[02:23:16.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003115)  
by the way, yung seeds at yung mga isda na linalagay dyan ay galing sa PA.

**[02:23:21.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003116)  
Sila nag-iprovide ng technical assistance.

**[02:23:24.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003117)  
Binibenta nila yan either through feeding program, through the Walang Gutom, or they consume it themselves.

**[02:23:29.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003118)  
So, somehow, yung panawagan ng DA na to do community gardens to ease prices ng pagkain is already underway.

**[02:23:38.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003119)  
We, in fact, have 5,000 of these sweeps nationwide.

**[02:23:42.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003120)  
Small water impounding projects.

**[02:23:45.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003121)  
Hindi ho yan nangyari overnight.

**[02:23:46.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003122)  
Pinaghandaan ho namin siya since 2023, kaya ganyan karami ho yan.

**[02:23:50.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003123)  
These sweeps are still...

**[02:23:51.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003124)  
active right now.

**[02:23:53.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003125)  
And the Department of Agriculture is helping us by introducing drought-resilient crops,

**[02:24:00.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003126)  
both high-value and regular, katulad ng taro, kamote, and pag high-value, watermelon, okra.

**[02:24:07.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003127)  
So, yan ho yung mga... yan yung partnership namin ng Department of Agriculture.

**[02:24:11.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003128)  
So, when we say resilient, so kasi we didn't look at El Nino just now,

**[02:24:15.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003129)  
but we were looking at El Nino way back in 2023, or La Nina as a matter of fact.

**[02:24:20.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003130)  
5,000 small water...

**[02:24:21.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003131)  
small water impounding systems or projects cannot be built in a span of couple of months.

**[02:24:26.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003132)  
That this was done way, way back.

**[02:24:28.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003133)  
Now, that's the first one in terms of social development.

**[02:24:31.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003134)  
We provide cash assistance.

**[02:24:32.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003135)  
By the way, baka matanong nyo anong connection namin doon?

**[02:24:34.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003136)  
Yung cash for work nila, for 20 days, they do the sweeps lang.

**[02:24:38.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003137)  
They do the impounding lang.

**[02:24:39.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003138)  
And then, kung ano yung inani doon sa kanila na yun, and then napapaikot na nila by themselves.

**[02:24:44.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003139)  
Now, on the second slide...

**[02:24:46.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003140)  
The second slide, obviously, yung bilanggit ni Sekiko kanina,

**[02:24:51.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003141)  
yung farmers na maapektuhan.

**[02:24:54.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003142)  
Nag-start na ho kami ng emergency cash transfers in Cagayan, in Isabela, Camarines Norte, as we speak.

**[02:25:01.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003143)  
Now, we are monitoring it based on the declarations of state of calamity.

**[02:25:05.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003144)  
And like Sekiko said, may close coordination kami, they will pass on to us areas of vulnerability.

**[02:25:11.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003145)  
Na tulad ng nangyari sa RSSI, they were the ones who told us which provinces to move into

**[02:25:16.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003146)  
based also on the state of calamity.

**[02:25:18.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003147)  
Next slide.

**[02:58:02.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004309)

**[02:58:09.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004310)  
Madam Chair, as mentioned, this is the successor program ng Kalahi na original that's been running for 20-25 years.

**[02:58:16.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004311)  
Nung 2024, nag-sunset na yung Kalahi.

**[02:58:21.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004312)  
Basically, ang programa nun.

**[02:58:23.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004313)  
And wala yan sa cities, wala yan sa first-class, second-class cities.

**[02:58:27.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004314)  
Normally, they're in the farthest, poorest provinces or municipalities that otherwise hindi mapapansin ng ga'a.

**[02:58:35.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004315)  
Kasi ang layo nila, nasa liblib na lugar.

**[02:58:38.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004316)  
Ang ginagawa ko dyan, the community identifies, parang bottom-up budgeting yan.

**[02:58:42.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004317)  
The community identifies the project that they need.

**[02:58:46.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004318)

**[02:58:52.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004319)  
Now,

**[02:58:53.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004320)

**[02:58:57.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004321)

**[02:59:01.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004322)

**[02:59:07.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004323)
