**[03:44:04.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005893)  
mentioned it.

**[03:44:04.860] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005894)  
Yes, I'm sorry.

**[03:44:05.620] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005895)  
It's okay.

**[03:44:06.340] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005896)  
And also for the others,

**[03:44:07.880] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005897)  
I was going to mention that

**[03:44:09.740] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005898)  
we also have the laws

**[03:44:11.100] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005899)  
which recognize persons

**[03:44:12.280] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005900)  
with rare diseases,

**[03:44:14.020] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005901)  
the mental health law,

**[03:44:15.920] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005902)  
the national cancer control law

**[03:44:17.700] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005903)  
which all recognizes

**[03:44:18.680] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005904)  
these persons with disabilities.

**[03:44:21.020] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005905)  
These constituencies

**[03:44:23.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005906)  
as persons with disabilities.

**[03:44:25.880] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005907)  
But we have specific questions

**[03:44:27.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005908)  
because we have many good policies

**[03:44:29.480] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005909)  
which unfortunately

**[03:44:30.400] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005910)  
remain on paper.

**[03:44:32.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005911)  
For instance,

**[03:44:32.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005912)  
the 1992 RA 7277

**[03:44:36.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005913)  
has a section on

**[03:44:37.560] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005914)  
auxiliary social services

**[03:44:39.100] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005915)  
which includes

**[03:44:40.220] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005916)  
the provision of

**[03:44:41.460] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005917)  
qualified sign language interpreters

**[03:44:43.340] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005918)  
as part of that.

**[03:44:45.040] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005919)  
And that has remained

**[03:44:46.320] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005920)  
unimplemented since 1992.

**[03:44:49.320] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005921)  
The DSWD,

**[03:44:51.020] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005922)  
actually issued

**[03:44:51.640] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005923)  
an administrative order

**[03:44:52.820] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005924)  
in 2003-059

**[03:44:54.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005925)  
for the guidelines

**[03:44:55.620] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005926)  
for the implementation

**[03:44:57.060] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005927)  
of the auxiliary social services

**[03:44:59.080] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005928)  
in coordination...

**[03:45:00.000] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005929)  
A more recent one is because of the previous 1% earmarked spending of all agencies during the second decade of persons with disabilities lapsed from 2003 to 2012.

**[03:45:17.080] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005930)  
So the general provision in the GAA annually has remained but with no more earmarking.

**[03:45:22.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005931)  
So it's just requiring agencies to have programs and activities for persons with disabilities and older persons.

**[03:45:31.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005932)  
In fact, there is a Joint Circular DBM-DSWD-NCDA-NCSC Joint Circular No. 1 Series of 2023 on the MEAL, the MEAL, Monitoring and Evaluation of Accountability for Learning.

**[03:45:49.080] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005933)  
For and learning from the implementation of Section 12.

**[03:45:52.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005934)  
So there has been a general provision 36. It was 37 in the succeeding years. It's been still in 2026.

**[03:46:08.040] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005935)  
And this is, and we requested actually this week, we asked the DSWD to send us some of the annual, the reports because of this Joint Circular.

**[03:46:21.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005936)  
And we were surprised.

**[03:46:22.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005937)  
We were surprised to receive tables showing shelters, facilities, and rehabilitation programs.

**[03:46:29.400] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005938)  
And so we were wondering, we weren't quite sure what was in their minds in looking at all of the, what was mandated in the general provision.

**[03:46:40.060] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005939)  
In the budget hearing last Monday, and I think Senator, the Honorable Senator Erwin Tufo mentioned this, our DSWD Secretary mentioned in relation to this,

**[03:46:50.980] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005940)  
we raised the,

**[03:46:52.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005941)  
the implemented sections of the Magna Carta.

**[03:46:57.480] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005942)  
And regarding particularly the auxiliary social services,

**[03:47:01.060] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005943)  
and our good Secretary mentioned that they have already proposed indicators for the SGLC to compel LGUs to comply.

**[03:47:12.780] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005944)  
And that has to do with my last point later on.

**[03:47:16.980] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005945)  
And also our...

**[03:47:18.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005946)  
I thought your last point was now.

**[03:47:20.360] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005947)  
Okay.

**[03:47:20.880] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005948)  
Okay.

**[03:47:22.800] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005949)  
N-C-D-A-E-D, Ms. Relova, and because we were asking even in the budget hearings of the House regarding Filipino Sign Language interpreters,

**[03:47:35.400] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005950)  
and they mentioned an upcoming or ongoing DSWD Academy training allotted for 1 million four times a year, 45 trainees, and that you already have 80 interpreters on your roster.

**[03:47:47.460] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005951)  
However, on the ground, we have received cases of rape victims.

**[03:47:52.360] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005952)  
And they're usually women and girls who are unschooled and therefore much more vulnerable who have been advised by the social workers to learn sign language first on their own so that they could have the communication skills.

**[03:48:05.880] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005953)  
Essentially, we feel this is highly discriminatory.

**[03:48:08.940] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005954)  
And especially so since the Supreme Court has already issued a rule on interpreting, specifically institutionalizing deaf-rely interpreters for unschooled deaf people.

**[03:48:21.560] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005955)  
So,

**[03:48:22.360] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005956)  
they may not sign in the standard language, but they are highly communicative.

**[03:48:29.240] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005957)  
So, we are very disturbed because the incidence of rape and sexual violence is about 10 times higher than the national average.

**[03:48:37.340] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005958)  
Last 30 seconds.

**[03:48:41.540] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005959)  
Last na po.

**[03:48:42.720] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005960)  
Here is just a challenge or food for thought.

**[03:48:47.660] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005961)  
We have been talking a lot since Monday about the PWD IDs.

**[03:48:51.180] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005962)  
Maybe it is time for us to step back a little and maybe not put too much value on the discounts and the VAT exemption if we can provide a whole of government approach in really providing services to persons with disabilities.

**[03:49:09.220] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005963)  
I can imagine that if there is no discount, then the government will have all agencies, not just DSWD na sila ang tinuturo lagi or DILG only.

**[03:49:20.520] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005964)  
But all agencies have to provide, make their public services accessible to all.

**[03:49:26.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005965)  
The reason that disability is such a beast to deal with siguro in governance, it's because it's a highly diverse group.

**[03:49:35.200] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005966)  
You're talking about, we have many conceptions of sino ba yung totoong person with a disability and laking debate niyan between the SWDD or HNCDA and so on.

**[03:49:48.060] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005967)  
Where are the deaf-blind?

**[03:49:49.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005968)  
Where are, we don't talk very often about those on the spectrum, those with intellectual disabilities, and those with mental health, those who have episodic and chronic conditions.

**[03:50:02.920] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005969)  
You don't see them.

**[03:50:04.880] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005970)  
And sometimes they're all just lumped.

**[03:50:06.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005971)  
O, psychosocial kayo lahat.

**[03:50:09.060] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005972)  
Okay.

**[03:50:10.120] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005973)  
And our experience with the deaf, it shows that this whole government approach is so critical.

**[03:50:17.320] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005974)  
And this is our appeal to the committee.

**[03:50:19.760] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005975)  
Let us not just put it all on one agency.

**[03:50:23.320] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005976)  
Because for instance, if we look at the 2,000 proposed disability support allowance, 2,000 per month, one interpreting for one hour is already 1,000.

**[03:50:34.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005977)  
Going to see a developmental pediatrician is at least 5,000.

**[03:50:39.020] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005978)  
Taking a grab, just to go from if you're in a wheelchair or you have to carry your child who has severe cerebral palsy,

**[03:50:48.820] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005979)  
will take you at least 800 pesos.

**[03:50:51.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005980)  
Wala ka agad yung 2,000.

**[03:50:57.360] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005981)  
There are now 35 deaf people who have been arrested without being read their rights.

**[03:51:05.780] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005982)  
They do not know why they're being arrested.

**[03:51:09.720] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005983)  
There was an attempt to translate the Miranda rights,

**[03:51:13.700] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005984)  
but it was done, unfortunately, without the lack of awareness.

**[03:51:17.860] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005985)  
That you have to ask deaf people.

**[03:51:18.820] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005986)  
So that they can be understood.

**[03:51:21.260] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005987)  
These are mostly deaf people who have never been to school.

**[03:51:24.780] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005988)  
The number is growing.

**[03:51:27.360] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005989)  
Where are we in the deaf-deaf SDGs?

**[03:51:31.980] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005990)  
The time will be up in three years.

**[03:51:34.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005991)  
We have had 15 years, SDG 1 on the poverty about leaving no one behind.

**[03:51:42.560] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005992)  
Persons with disabilities and children are already left behind.

**[03:51:45.500] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005993)  
Because there's no data even up until now.

**[03:51:48.820] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005994)  
How much is government spending for persons with disabilities, including children with disabilities?

**[03:51:54.660] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005995)  
The only study which has been done was in 2013 by Civil Society, the Philippine Coalition on the CRPD.

**[03:52:01.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005996)  
But as of now, we don't know.

**[03:52:03.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005997)  
We are invisible.

**[03:52:05.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-005998)  
Okay, this is the final.

**[03:52:07.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005999)  
Yes, please, because also Senator Amy would actually like to interject here.

**[03:52:11.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006000)  
Okay, so please wrap up, Ms. Liza.

**[03:52:13.160] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006001)  
This is a provision which builds on, this is for the deaf,

**[03:52:17.860] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006002)  
and the main point, it has several things, we have sent the draft,

**[03:52:23.900] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006003)  
is to make persons with disabilities visible.

**[03:52:28.100] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006004)  
We are not asking for any appropriation, but we are asking for certain accounting,

**[03:52:34.780] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006005)  
you know, governance mechanisms, just so that it can be seen.

**[03:52:39.260] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006006)  
Because if you're out of sight, you're out of mind.

**[03:52:42.460] Dr. Liza Martinez** (asr-006007)  
That's all. Thank you very much.

**[03:52:43.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006008)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[03:52:44.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006009)  
Thank you very much, Ms. Liza.

**[03:52:45.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006010)  
Yes, for the Chair.

**[03:52:47.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006011)  
Senator, I'm...

**[03:52:47.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006012)  
Dr. Martinez's plea that, in fact, those with severe challenges should actually be provided their own stipend.

**[03:52:57.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006013)  
I filed in the last Congress still a disability support fund already and refiled it in 2025 last year.

**[03:53:07.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006014)  
But we had discussions.

**[03:53:09.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006015)  
It's a far more modest proposition at 500 bucks per month, which is hardly enough, as you said,

**[03:53:14.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006016)  
because it's extremely expensive, assistive devices.

**[03:53:17.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006017)  
Therapy, et cetera, is already quite onerous.

**[03:53:21.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006018)  
And we came to some kind of agreement at that point in time, sa 500 a month lang muna.

**[03:53:28.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006019)  
Kasi kailangan mag-identify ka ng funding source.

**[03:53:31.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006020)  
Hindi ba, alam ni Chair yun.

**[03:53:33.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006021)  
At sana mabuhay ngayon, and I appreciate hearing it from you,

**[03:53:38.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006022)  
because nanahimik na yung bilis.

**[03:53:40.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006023)  
Sana buhayin natin ulit.

**[03:53:42.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006024)  
At tigit sa lahat, very alarming nga ang criminal statistics,

**[03:53:46.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006025)  
yung mga crimes against chastity.

**[03:53:47.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006026)  
And sexual assault sa ating deaf community.

**[03:53:53.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006027)  
And also the blind community.

**[03:53:55.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006028)  
Talagang malaking problema yan.

**[03:53:57.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006029)  
At kinakailangan talaga pagtuunan ng pansin.

**[03:54:01.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006030)  
Pero in the SWD, severely hampered, as are the LGUs,

**[03:54:05.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006031)  
for lack na nga of the psychologists, psychiatrists, and others who can identify mental

**[03:54:12.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006032)  
and the cover, catch-all, psychosocial neurodivergence,

**[03:54:17.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006033)  
divergent, etc., classification.

**[03:54:21.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006034)  
Salamat, Sen. Amy.

**[03:54:22.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006035)  
Before I ask the Secretary if he'd like to respond initially dun sa inputs ni Ms. Liza,

**[03:54:29.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006036)  
gusto kong i-confirm na meron nga ang mga disability support allowances bills filed.

**[03:54:38.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006037)  
Yung sa representasyong ito, 2,000 a month din ang proposal sa bill.

**[03:54:44.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006038)  
So, pagtutulungan na lang namin i-follow up.

**[03:54:47.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006039)  
Muli.

**[03:54:47.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006040)  
Muli itong kongreso yung proposals namin iyon.

**[03:54:52.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006041)  
Ang ganda po nung proposal about a predictable rights-based social protection.

**[03:54:58.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006042)  
And sinare ko rin kay Secrex nung mga briefings namin na gusto ko talaga ma-raise yung profile ng department

**[03:55:05.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006043)  
aside from for their essential, urgent, tactical responses, yung long-term strategic trajectory ng department.

**[03:55:15.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006044)  
And I think isang magandang possible anchor ito.

**[03:55:17.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006045)  
Yung rights-based social protection na ang DSWD ang mag-champion yan.

**[03:55:22.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006046)  
So, well-noted yung mga specific proposed, possible proposed amendments for 2027 na i-restore to at least 2026 levels

**[03:55:32.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006047)  
at o i-adopt yung Tier 2.

**[03:55:35.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006048)  
And then yung possible amendment on the child protection system.

**[03:55:42.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006049)  
Kaugnay din ito sa kapapasa din naming batang ligtas.

**[03:55:47.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006050)  
Law, no?

**[03:55:48.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006051)  
Laban nga sa OSAIC, CISAEM, at pati laban sa NVE.

**[03:55:51.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006052)  
And then yun na nga po yung disability support allowance.

**[03:55:54.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006053)  
So, Secrex, would you like to respond?

**[03:55:56.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006054)  
Well-noted, Madam Chair.

**[03:55:58.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006055)  
Actually, Social Watch Philippines is a partner of ours in the cabinet cluster on social development and social protection.

**[03:56:08.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006056)  
So, all the points that they raise are well-noted.

**[03:56:10.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006057)  
I just want to put on record na meron na ho tayong mga incubator social protection programs in those areas.

**[03:56:17.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006058)  
At least that you mentioned.

**[03:56:19.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006059)  
First of all is yung binalik sa house na budget na 25 million for Project Aruga.

**[03:56:24.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006060)  
Project Aruga is a pilot project that takes care of the family, the caregivers themselves, as well as the child na may disability.

**[03:56:32.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006061)  
So, it calls for support in early detection, assessments.

**[03:56:37.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006062)  
Number two, meron ng disability support allowance na TIG to 2,000.

**[03:56:41.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006063)  
And then they also get auxiliary services.

**[03:56:43.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006064)  
We're going to use our AICs to tie up with service providers in the area.

**[03:56:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006065)  
So that whatever therapy or whatever intervention that they need, especially for children with special needs,

**[03:56:54.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006066)  
can seek help using our guarantee letters ng AICs program, another use of the AICs program.

**[03:57:00.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006067)  
And then it also calls for, it also has a built-in capacity building for family support

**[03:57:06.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006068)  
because it can be taxing for the family of a person with disability on how to cope.

**[03:57:12.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006069)  
We also have to take care of the family themselves.

**[03:57:14.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006070)  
And last, advocacy campaigns.

**[03:57:17.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006071)  
A lot of people are still ignorant, not because they choose to do so,

**[03:57:20.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006072)  
but because of lack of information on what they should and not do,

**[03:57:24.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006073)  
what they should say and what not say,

**[03:57:26.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006074)  
or how they should refer to people with disabilities.

**[03:57:29.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006075)  
So, the whole program is holistic.

**[03:57:31.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006076)  
Sa HGAB po, nabalik na ho siya, 25 million.

**[03:57:34.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006077)  
Malit lang ho siya, but it's a good starting point.

**[03:57:37.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006078)  
These are pilot programs in selected LGUs that we can learn from

**[03:57:41.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006079)  
so that pagka time na may budget na mas malaki,

**[03:57:45.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006080)  
we can already have the learnings and the impact.

**[03:57:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006081)  
The assessments and the monitorings.

**[03:57:49.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006082)  
Pangalawa po, the other fund that was restored was the 25 million fund for Project SAFE,

**[03:57:56.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006083)  
which is an OSAIC advocacy campaign.

**[03:57:59.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006084)  
One, it teaches people or the young people on what to look out for in their families,

**[03:58:04.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006085)  
the red flags.

**[03:58:06.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006086)  
Pangalawa, it has a strong reporting system and network of care for OSAIC victims.

**[03:58:12.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006087)  
Pangatlo, obviously what we do best are healing services in our care facilities.

**[03:58:17.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006088)  
And pangapat, recovery and reintegration services.

**[03:58:22.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006089)  
So, it's again a pilot because of the tight fiscal space,

**[03:58:25.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006090)  
but at least nandun na ho siya, the 25 million.

**[03:58:27.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006091)  
Now, I totally agree with the good professor on restoring sana the attached agency budgets

**[03:58:35.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006092)  
because these are administrative in nature.

**[03:58:37.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006093)  
As mentioned to the good chairwoman,

**[03:58:39.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006094)  
marami ho doon are employee-based na compensation na maaring mawala by next year

**[03:58:45.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006095)  
kung hindi ho maibabalik yung mga points.

**[03:58:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006096)  
Now, we can do a better job with the implementation of FSL.

**[03:58:54.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006097)  
We admit that, we admitted that in the House.

**[03:58:58.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006098)  
Kaming nga mismo sa Departamento, kulang ho kami.

**[03:59:01.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006099)  
Now, what is the concrete step towards the direction that you want?

**[03:59:05.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006100)  
The Department has an academy.

**[03:59:06.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006101)  
It's about to be opened by the end of the year.

**[03:59:08.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006102)  
Of course, we're running it off-site now.

**[03:59:11.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006103)  
The academy will work with the Philippine Association of the Deaf

**[03:59:14.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006104)  
to help capacitate our own service providers.

**[03:59:17.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006105)  
As well as LGU service providers

**[03:59:19.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006106)  
by doing these courses inside our academy, these certification programs.

**[03:59:25.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006107)  
Kung kami nga ho mismo, like I said, we can do a better job,

**[03:59:28.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006108)  
then admittedly, we should be doing a better job.

**[03:59:32.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006109)  
Salamat, Secrex.

**[03:59:34.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006110)  
Can I just wrap up, Madam Chair?

**[03:59:36.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006111)  
Yes, of course, please.

**[03:59:37.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006112)  
Now, yung 1%, we feel strongly about that.

**[03:59:40.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006113)  
That GMC came to life because we wanted to make sure

**[03:59:44.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006114)  
na may mga positions in government

**[03:59:46.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006115)  
that are reserved for people with disability.

**[03:59:49.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006116)  
Pero tama ho kayo nung sinubmit yung mga plano.

**[03:59:52.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006117)  
Sometimes you'd feel that they're haphazardly done.

**[03:59:54.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006118)  
That's why I spoke to Secretary Kim

**[03:59:56.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006119)  
na we're going to do a budget converging.

**[04:00:00.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006120)  
planning, meaning

**[04:00:01.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006121)

**[04:00:04.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006122)

**[04:00:06.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006123)

**[04:00:08.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006124)

**[04:00:10.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006125)  
Kayo nga nagbanggit kung parang

**[04:00:12.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006126)  
feeling nyo kung ano-ano na lang yung nilagay

**[04:00:14.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006127)  
doon. So we totally agree.

**[04:00:16.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006128)  
Give us the time to work with DBM

**[04:00:22.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006131)

but to also make sure that our workplaces

**[04:00:26.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006133)

**[04:00:28.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006134)  
and conducive

**[04:00:30.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006135)  
to working for people with disabilities.

**[04:01:46.380] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006172)

**[04:01:50.160] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006174)

**[04:01:51.900] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006175)

**[04:01:53.840] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006176)

**[04:01:55.880] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006177)

**[04:02:06.560] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006182)  
Pero, sana

**[04:02:07.660] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006183)

**[04:02:10.740] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006184)  
Then, pangatlo po,

**[04:02:13.000] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006185)

Then, isa po sa mga partner LGUs

**[04:02:17.900] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006188)

**[04:02:20.060] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006189)

**[04:02:21.800] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006190)

**[04:02:23.940] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006191)  
Dahil po dun sa CBMS ngayon,

**[04:02:26.600] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006192)  
um, para

**[04:02:27.800] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006193)  
ang, ang

**[04:02:29.220] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006194)

**[04:02:31.780] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006195)

**[04:02:33.580] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006196)

**[04:02:35.880] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006197)

**[04:02:37.700] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006198)

**[04:02:39.680] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006199)

**[04:02:41.800] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006200)

**[04:02:43.940] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006201)

**[04:02:45.640] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006202)

**[04:02:47.680] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006203)

**[04:02:49.620] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006204)  
database po for senior citizen, yung

**[04:02:51.780] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006205)  
senior citizens registration

**[04:02:54.040] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006206)  
and information system na

**[04:02:55.940] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006207)  
actually, ano na po rin,

**[04:02:57.760] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006208)  
namin to sa NCSC para magamit

**[04:02:59.680] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006209)  
po sana, na-focus po talaga sa senior

**[04:03:01.560] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006210)  
citizens. So, nasa OSCA siya

**[04:03:03.660] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006211)  
or MSWDO, CSWDO,

**[04:03:05.960] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006212)

**[04:03:07.480] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006213)

**[04:03:09.600] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006214)

**[04:03:11.420] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006215)  
ma-i-enroll po agad. So, mas

**[04:03:13.400] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006216)

**[04:03:14.900] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006217)

**[04:03:17.460] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006218)

Thank you rin, Ms.

**[04:03:21.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006220)  
Emily.

**[04:03:22.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006220-split-2)  
Madam

**[04:03:23.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006221)  
Chair, ma'am, points of clarification.

**[04:03:25.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006222)  
Yung CBMS, hindi po,

**[04:03:27.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006223)  
hindi po siya age-based, no?

**[04:03:30.000] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006224)  
So,

**[04:03:30.250] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006224-split-2)  
it covers everybody?

**[04:03:31.600] COSE Executive Director Emily N. Beridico** (asr-006225)  
Yes, po.

**[04:03:32.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006225-split-2)  
Well,

**[04:03:33.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006226)  
4.2 po yung target beneficiaries

**[04:03:35.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006227)  
this year, because yun po yung budget na

**[04:03:37.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006228)  
available. Pero sana nga parehas

**[04:03:39.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006229)  
kami na kasama nyo kami sa

**[04:03:41.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006230)  
lahat universal.

**[04:03:43.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006231)  
But number two, yung CBMS doesn't

**[04:03:45.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006232)  
exclude based on age. Pero

**[04:03:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006233)  
adminado rin po ang PSA and the good

**[04:03:49.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006234)  
chair can validate this, na medyo malaki nga yung

**[04:03:51.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006235)  
naging exclusion because it probably was the

**[04:03:53.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006236)  
first time that we were running CBMS nationally.

**[04:03:56.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006237)  
Now, ang panawa,

**[04:03:57.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006238)  
yung advice namin sa mga LGU, at alam

**[04:03:59.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006239)  
na rin nila ito, na dahil lahat po ng

**[04:04:01.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006240)  
financial assistance, not just from the

**[04:04:03.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006241)  
SWD, dahan-dahan na

**[04:04:05.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006242)  
nakinukuha sa CBMS,

**[04:04:07.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006243)  
seryosohin nila. You have to remember, ako ho,

**[04:04:09.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006244)  
nag-imero kasi ako. The CBMS is only as good as

**[04:04:11.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006245)  
the support from the local government unit.

**[04:04:13.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006246)  
Kasi if you are not going to monitor

**[04:04:15.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006247)  
the enumerators, talagang hindi yan makakapasok

**[04:04:17.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006248)  
kung saan-saan. And hindi lang naman ho ito sa

**[04:04:19.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006249)

**[04:04:22.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006250)  
So,

**[04:04:23.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006251)  
I think our local government units will do better

**[04:04:25.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006252)  
now in monitoring the enumerators

**[04:04:27.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006253)  
kasi sila ang magiging

**[04:04:29.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006254)  
biktima sa dulo, mga constituent nila.

**[04:04:31.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006255)  
Now, Madam, you mentioned

**[04:04:33.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006256)  
na may mga 60 pa taas na hindi pa

**[04:04:35.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006257)  
nakakatanggap. As mentioned a while ago kasi,

**[04:04:37.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006258)  
we reformatted the guidelines in the

**[04:04:39.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006259)  
department na dahil limitado yung pera,

**[04:04:41.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006260)  
binaliktad natin, unahin natin yung mas

**[04:04:43.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006261)  
matatanda, paatras. Kasi

**[04:04:45.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006262)  
for obvious reasons na hindi

**[04:04:47.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006263)  
natin sure kung baka

**[04:04:49.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006264)  
anytime, hindi na sila

**[04:04:51.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006265)  
tumagal. So, that's why pinaatras natin.

**[04:04:53.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006266)  
Meaning, 100, pababa muna.

**[04:04:55.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006267)  
That's why you will probably see

**[04:04:57.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006268)  
like that sa mga LGU na bakit

**[04:04:59.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006269)  
mas marami sa 60, tapos

**[04:05:01.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006270)  
yung mga 70 wala, pero mas marami nakakatanggap

**[04:05:03.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006271)  
dun sa matatanda. Binaliktad

**[04:05:05.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006272)  
ho namin yun. Now,

**[04:05:07.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006273)  
lastly, I know

**[04:05:09.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006274)  
the intention of a database

**[04:05:11.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006275)  
for the seniors is good. But

**[04:05:13.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006276)  
alam po nyo, from my experience

**[04:05:15.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006277)  
as mayor and now

**[04:05:17.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006278)  
as secretary, multiple databases

**[04:05:19.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006279)
